# L01 staged: every room furnished

Generated by `staging_report.py` from `layouts/` and `catalog/items/`; re-run it after any change. Every room but the kitchen has a staged layout (**A**) and alternatives (**B**, **C**, **D**); open them in the planner (https://yizyace.github.io/vesta-roomscape-v1/) and switch with the letters in the toolbar. Prices are IKEA US / Amazon list prices as read on 2026-10-22, 2026-10-23, 2026-10-24, 2026-10-25 and 2026-10-26, each item's own date in its catalog file (rules.md F-02); nothing has been bought.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Staged pick, re-staged 2026-10-26 on Andrew's IKEA cart: the MORABO Sofa with chaise ($1,199, one article: a loveseat frame and a chaise section) in place of the U (the 5-seat corner and the chaise add-on, $2,449; f keeps that room), facing the window as before with the chaise at the stairs end; the play area and the lamps are unchanged, and the floor between the sofa's end and the play rug opens to 56.5". No TV (F-65); the bottom side is open to the kitchen and dining area, so its 30-inch walkway (y 143-173) stays clear (F-42): nothing stands against it or hangs on it, and it is the route from the stairs to the kitchen and dining room.
The sofa (F-67, Andrew 2026-10-25: "a chaise, i.e. at least 3 seats", "empty space between like" the MORABO chaise add-on, "the largest sofa / sectional / chaise that can fit"): MORABO in Gunnared dark gray on 6 1/4" wood legs, the open base he linked, high enough for a robot vacuum. Its cover is fixed (vacuum and wipe, not washable); the same pieces in Grann/Bomstad leather wipe clean and hold no dust mites (F-66). The sofa is delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22): Andrew can't tape the stairs before the move (2026-10-26, F-07). It is in his cart to order on 2026-10-26, his call: its boxes are the loveseat frame's, 69 x 37 x 21" at 115 lb 12 oz, and the chaise section's, 64 1/2 x 32 1/4 x 21 1/2" at 69 lb 4 oz, each smaller than the U's corner frame (78 1/2 x 36 3/4 x 20 1/4"); the cart page says returns within 365 days.
His pick (2026-10-26) over the largest that fits: the MORABO Sofa with chaise, 96 1/8" across and 62 1/4" deep at the chaise, three seats (IKEA counts the chaise as one) around the VALNÄS, floating with its back on the open side's walkway edge and facing the window. The U it replaces (141 3/4 x 95 1/4", f) took the floor to x 177.75; this sofa ends at x 132.125, so the open floor between it and the play rug is 56.5" (it was 10.9" beside the U's arm).
sofa-1 MORABO Sofa with chaise, rot 180, x 36-132.125, y 80.75-143: the loveseat part (x 66.75-132.125, y 106.75-143) faces the window with the 30" walkway behind it (F-39, F-42); the chaise (x 36-66.75, the full 62 1/4") at the stairs end, on the edge of the railing's 36" strip (F-40), its lounger toward the window, its foot 74.75" from the heater face (F-33); delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22). The chaise section can be built on either end (the loose armrest goes on its outer side); drawn on the right seen from the front, by the stairs, so the loveseat's end is toward the play floor and from the seats you see Maddie past it. D turns the same sofa to face the play area.
coffee-table-1 VALNÄS oak ellipse, x 80.875-127.375, y 62.375-90.75: 16" from the loveseat's front, 14.125" from the chaise's side (F-39), open on the play side; no corners at Maddie's head height. The seats are reached past the table's play-side end or through the 14" gap at the chaise, as at any coffee table.
wall-lamp-3 VARPTROSS (top), on the window wall at x 50-57: the group's lamp on the wall, a soft glow (5.9 W), with the ceiling light and the lamp on the VALNÄS for reading; its cord in a cover down the wall to an outlet above the heater, never across it (final audit 2026-10-26: the TÅGARP that stood here, 16.5" off the wall, could reach a window-wall outlet only with its 3' 3" cord lying over the heater, F-33). plant-1 FEJKA bird of paradise at the sofa's play-side end, x 133-142, y 134-143, 7/8" off the arm on the walkway's edge (F-42), where it marks the group's edge without standing in a route. The play corner's UPPLYST, the VARPTROSS over the BESTÅ and wall-lamp-4, a second VARPTROSS over the reading bench as its reading light (final audit 2026-10-26), light the right side.
rug-1 TIOKRONA 118 x 94", x 36-154, y 29.5-123.5: under the VALNÄS, the chaise's outer and front legs and the loveseat's front legs, running 22" past the sofa's end toward the play floor (its west edge on the railing strip's edge); two STOPP FILT underlays keep it put. Its page says "Do not wash" and never a rotating brush: the jute can't take the robot vacuum the open base was chosen for (F-67), so give the robot a no-go zone over it, or swap it for a washable flatweave of about 118 x 94" (none in the catalog).
Play area (F-61), as staged before: toy-bench-1 SMÅSTAD reading bench in the window corner (x 240.5-261, y 18-53.4) with wall-lamp-1 UPPLYST over it; toy-storage-1 TROFAST below it (x 243.6-261, y 56.4-93), 24" clear in front (F-32), art-1 PJÄTTERYD above; toy-cabinet-1 BESTÅ with doors on the right-lower wall (x 259-275.5, y 94.75-142), its FLISAT book display on top and wall-lamp-2 VARPTROSS above, art-2 RÖDALM; rug-3 LEN play rug (x 183-235, y 75-138), 29" of bare floor from the TIOKRONA; kids-table-1 LÄTT and its two chairs on it (x 188.6-213.4, y 98-116.9), 56.5" from the sofa's end and 30.7" on the diagonal from its corner to the TROFAST's corner (243.6, 93), the way from the kitchen side to the play wall (F-30).
Walkways: the open side's walkway (F-42); the route along the window from the top landing to the play corner, 74.75" wide at the chaise's foot and open beyond the sofa (F-30, F-40); from the kitchen side up to the play wall past the LÄTT, 30.7" between its corner and the TROFAST's (F-30), and 56.5" between the sofa's end and the LÄTT; the bottom landing onto the walkway; the sofa opens toward the window and the play side. Nowhere to set a cup from the chaise (the VALNÄS is 29" from its lounger): a clip-on tray over the chaise's arm would do it without taking floor (not in the catalog).
Not placed: the MORABO U, the 5-seat corner and the chaise add-on (f: the largest that fits, until Andrew's cart chose this sofa); the KIVIK sofa (rejected: no chaise, no open base, F-67); the POÄNG (an armchair would sit in the route along the window); the LACK console and its STORSEGEL (it stood against the old sofa's back, now on the walkway edge) and the side table with its STORSEGEL (no wall beside the U for a lamp's cord); the TÅGARPs (one stood by the window until the final audit 2026-10-26, its cord over the heater; the other, right of the window, in front of the reading bench); the TV and its units (F-65); the LILJETRÄD mirror (glass, F-21).
Stairs: beyond the left wall behind a railing (F-40), so the 36" strip along it (x 0-36) stays clear its whole length, landings included; the bottom landing opens onto the open side's walkway, the top one onto the floor along the window (F-30). Which end people step off is unknown.
Heater: clearances run from the drawn 6" heater face (heater-depth-assumed): floor pieces from y 12, upholstery from y 18 (F-33). It runs the whole window wall, so the GINSTMOTT panels must stay off it: hem them to end above it once its height is known, or hang blinds as the office does. Window: eyeballed on the top wall (assumed-position:window); nothing stands under it (F-38).
Power: outlets can only be on the window wall and the play wall, and where they are isn't recorded; the heater runs the whole window wall, so a cord to an outlet there must come down the wall in a cover from a wall lamp, never across the heater from a floor lamp (final audit 2026-10-26: the TÅGARP came off for that reason), and the UPPLYST and VARPTROSS cords on the play wall run down it in covers.
Kid safety (F-21): no glass; the FLISAT book display stands on the 15" BESTÅ, 31" together, so fix it to the wall; nothing else is over 27"; the VARPTROSS hangs out of her reach.
Extras: GINSTMOTT curtains x2 pairs, HUGAD rod x1, SVARTPOPPEL covers x2, VALLKRASSING covers x2, FJÄDRAR inserts x4, VALLKRASSING throw x1, LÖVA canopy x1 over the reading bench, STOPP FILT underlays x2 (under the TIOKRONA).
Total $2,656.76: placed $2,408.86 + extras $247.90; prices as of 2026-10-22 to 2026-10-26 (those before today price-stale, F-02). Flags across the layout: delivery-path-unmeasured, assumed-position:window, heater-depth-assumed.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the LÄTT moved 11.4" west, so the way from the kitchen side to the play wall is 30.7" (both ways past it were under 30": 22.25" beside the U's arm and 19.5" to the TROFAST's corner), the LEN with it, and the TIOKRONA 9" west so the whole chaise stands on it; the TÅGARP became a VARPTROSS on the window wall, since its cord would have crossed the heater to reach any outlet there (F-33); a second VARPTROSS over the reading bench is the play wall's reading light.

</details>

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Alternative: Andrew's "chaise addon to sofa + love seat" (2026-10-25). No TV (F-65); the open side's walkway (y 143-173) stays clear (F-42).
The sofa (F-67, Andrew 2026-10-25: "a chaise, i.e. at least 3 seats", "empty space between like" the MORABO chaise add-on, "the largest sofa / sectional / chaise that can fit"): MORABO in Gunnared dark gray on 6 1/4" wood legs, the open base he linked, high enough for a robot vacuum. Its cover is fixed (vacuum and wipe, not washable); the same pieces in Grann/Bomstad leather wipe clean and hold no dust mites (F-66). Every sofa piece is delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22): Andrew can't tape the stairs before the move (2026-10-26, F-07), so the sofa is ordered after move-in day's tape and the beds go first; the largest box is the corner's frame, 78 1/2 x 36 3/4 x 20 1/4".
The sofa with its chaise floats on the walkway edge facing the window, and the loveseat along the railing's strip faces the play area, so whoever sits there watches Maddie. Five seats and the chaise.
loveseat-1 MORABO loveseat, rot 270, x 36-72.25, y 41.375-106.75: on the edge of the railing's 36" strip (F-40), facing east; its north end 35.4" from the heater face, the top landing's exit along the window, with nothing on the floor beside it (F-40, F-30); delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22).
sofa-1 MORABO sofa, rot 180, x 72.25-153.375, y 106.75-143: facing the window, its back on the walkway edge (F-42, F-39 30" behind it); delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22).
chaise-1 MORABO chaise add-on, rot 180, x 153.375-184.125, y 80.75-143: at the sofa's right end (its armrest moves to the chaise's outer side), the lounger toward the window, 74.75" from the heater face (F-33); delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22).
side-table-1 LACK side table, x 50.6-72.25, y 106.75-128.4, touching the loveseat's south arm and the sofa's left arm (until the final audit 2026-10-26 it stood 7.25" and 10.6" off them with the plant between), a drink table for both, its 17 3/4" top 3/4" under the 18 1/2" seats; plant-1 FEJKA below it in the corner, x 40-49, y 125-134, 4" off the railing strip and 9" off the walkway.
coffee-table-1 VALNÄS, x 88.25-134.75, y 62.375-90.75: 16" from the loveseat and from the sofa, 18.6" from the chaise's side (F-39).
wall-lamp-3 VARPTROSS (top), on the window wall at x 50-57: the group's lamp. A floor lamp against that wall would narrow the top landing's exit beside the loveseat to 20" (F-40), so the light hangs on the wall; it is a soft glow (5.9 W), with the ceiling light for reading.
rug-1 TIOKRONA, x 52.5-170.5, y 29.5-123.5: under the VALNÄS, the loveseat's and the sofa's front legs and the chaise's inner legs (its outer legs 13.6" off it: the group is 148" wide against a 118" rug, so one end straddles); STOPP FILT underlays. Jute takes no robot vacuum (its page: never a rotating brush), so a no-go zone or a washable flatweave (F-67).

Rug-free alternative (F-66): the staged room (a; the MORABO Sofa with chaise since Andrew's cart of 2026-10-26) piece for piece without the TIOKRONA and the LEN, for a family that may skip rugs for dust and dust mites. No TV (F-65); the open side's walkway (y 143-173) stays clear (F-42).
The sofa (F-67, Andrew 2026-10-25: "a chaise, i.e. at least 3 seats", "empty space between like" the MORABO chaise add-on, "the largest sofa / sectional / chaise that can fit"): MORABO in Gunnared dark gray on 6 1/4" wood legs, the open base he linked, high enough for a robot vacuum. Its cover is fixed (vacuum and wipe, not washable); the same pieces in Grann/Bomstad leather wipe clean and hold no dust mites (F-66). The sofa is delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22): Andrew can't tape the stairs before the move (2026-10-26, F-07). It is in his cart to order on 2026-10-26, his call: its boxes are the loveseat frame's, 69 x 37 x 21" at 115 lb 12 oz, and the chaise section's, 64 1/2 x 32 1/4 x 21 1/2" at 69 lb 4 oz, each smaller than the U's corner frame (78 1/2 x 36 3/4 x 20 1/4"); the cart page says returns within 365 days.
What replaced each rug: the TIOKRONA under the conversation group gives way to the sofa itself, which holds the VALNÄS between its chaise and its seats; the LEN play rug gives way to two cork mats on natural rubber side by side (play-mat-1 and play-mat-2, 52 x 80" together, x 188-240, y 52-132), wipeable and plastic-free (Andrew, 2026-10-25: "something that isn't literally plastic"), in place of the GREJSIMOJS, whose cover is polyester with a polyurethane coat. No plastic-free mat came bigger: the 72 x 48" cork mats have plastic (TPE) backs. The rubber backs grip the floor, so the pair stays put; the LÄTT stands on the left mat (it straddled the seam until the final audit 2026-10-26).
F-66 moves: (1) the sofa around one anchor, the VALNÄS, 16" from its seats and 14.125" from the chaise; (2) light marking each zone: a VARPTROSS on the window wall for the group (the TÅGARP came off at the final audit 2026-10-26: its cord would have crossed the heater), the UPPLYST and a second VARPTROSS over the reading bench and the VARPTROSS over the BESTÅ for the play corner; (3) wall anchors on the play wall, the PJÄTTERYD over the TROFAST and the RÖDALM over the BESTÅ (the sofa faces the window, which stays the focal point); (4) the palette off the floor in machine-washable cushion covers, the throw and the curtains; (5) the FEJKA at the arm's end; (6) the cork mats on the play floor.
sofa-1, coffee-table-1, wall-lamp-3, plant-1: as in a (F-33, F-39, F-40, F-42 the same; delivery-path-unmeasured for the sofa, F-22). As in a, the sofa ends 56.5" short of the LÄTT, with open floor between the group and the play area.

Not placed: the TIOKRONA, the LEN and the GREJSIMOJS (F-66); the rest as in a.

The largest that fits: IKEA's MORABO 6-seat crn/chaise, a U 141 3/4" across and 95 1/4" deep, built from its two parts in the fabric Andrew linked (the one-piece article comes only in leather, ikea-09575866). It floats with its back on the open side's walkway edge and faces the window: five seats and the chaise (IKEA's "6-seat" counts the chaise) around the VALNÄS, a conversation pit that leaves the play floor on the right 66" wide. Anything bigger would take the play area (F-61) or the route along the window.

sofa-1 MORABO 5-seat corner, rot 180, x 66.75-177.75, y 47.75-143: the back run (y 106.75-143) faces the window with the 30" walkway behind it (F-39); the 95 1/4" arm on the right (x 141.5-177.75) faces west into the U, its end 41.75" from the heater face (F-33, 12" needed); delivery-path-unmeasured (F-22). <details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

The top side is open to the living room (Andrew, 2026-10-24) as well as the left side to the kitchen, so both 30-inch walkways stay clear (F-42): every floor piece stands at x 30 or more and y 30 or more, nothing hangs on either open side, and rugs run under the walkways.
Staged pick: the VIHALS / VIHALS white set for six (five VIHALS chairs and Maddie's URBAN), table closed and turned across the room (long side along the 128" walls), the one way its six seats fit: along the depth the bottom end would keep only 23.3" to the wall. The table sits in the open corner: its kitchen-end chair and its two living-room-side chairs stand just inside the walkways, so the walkways are the passing space behind them (F-36: 48.5" from the left end to the kitchen line and 48.5" from the top edge to the living-room line).
F-36 to the walls: 41.4" from the bottom edge to the bottom wall and 32.75" to the buffet's face, which floats above the chair backs (tight:32.8: the chairs push back under it, and nobody passes behind them, so the buffet is reached from its ends; it was 27.4" until the final audit 2026-10-26 moved the set 5.4" toward the living room), 32.25" from the right end to the right wall (tight:32.3, Maddie's end: a parent pushes the URBAN in). Leaf in (70 7/8"), the table opens 11.8" at each end: 20.4" from its right end to the wall (tight:20.4), so on guest evenings seat three to a side and leave both ends empty: nothing stands in the kitchen walkway (F-42), and Maddie's URBAN goes on the living-room side, where a parent can stand behind her; six placemats fit on the table only with the leaf in.
The view: from the living room you look across the table to the BLODFLÄDER over the buffet; from the kitchen, along the table to the bird of paradise in the far corner.
Audit 2026-10-24 (from the rug-free E): the wall lamps moved 1.4" outward (lamp-1 to x 39.1, lamp-2 to x 98.2), each centered between the canvas and the buffet's end; at 1" from the frame they crowded the canvas. Guest evenings with the leaf in seat three to a side, both ends empty, so no chair stands in the kitchen walkway; the HAVSRIS cord drop goes in a cord cover, never across an open side or behind Maddie's seat.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the set moved 5.4" toward the living room (the table's top edge at y 48.5, chairs 1 and 2 backing onto the walkway's edge like the kitchen-end chair), which took the buffet's face from 27.4" to 32.75" off the table and the bottom wall to 41.4"; the rug moved with it, still 24.9" past each long side. The HAVSRIS cord is 15' 5" (185"): with the ceiling unmeasured, check for a ceiling box first, and measure the ceiling and find the bottom wall's outlets before buying it (its drop and run need about 171" at a 96" ceiling and about 195" at 108", F-23).

</details>

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Rug-free (F-66): the staged arrangement (a) with the LYDERSHOLM rug and its two STOPP FILT underlays taken out; every other piece stands where it does in a, except the two wall lamps, moved in the audit below. Nothing replaces the rug on the floor. The tile wipes clean, and designers often leave a dining table bare anyway: chairs catch on a rug's edge, and with a 4-year-old at the table spills come off tile, not out of a flatweave.
What ties the room together instead:
(1) The table is the anchor, its six seats tight around it.
(2) The GULLSUDARE pendant hangs over the table's center (72.1, 63.1) and marks the dining zone the rug used to mark; the two VARPTROSS wall lamps frame the buffet.
(3) The wall anchor is the BLODFLÄDER, 47 1/4" wide, centered over the 70 7/8" BESTÅ buffet (two thirds of its width): the focal point from the living room.
(4) The rug's natural tone moves up onto the table and chairs: the washable SVARTSENAP runner and placemats and the MALINDA chair pads in natural and light beige, repeated in the bamboo of the VARPTROSS shades and the BLANDA MATT bowl, with the white of the VIHALS set and the buffet. The same natural, white and green run through the living room, so the open plan still reads as one space.
(5) The FEJKA bird of paradise in the far corner carries the green of the painting. An artificial plant gathers dust, so it is wiped with the dusting.
Clearances are as in a. Both 30-inch walkways stay clear (F-42). F-36: 48.5" to the kitchen line and 48.5" to the living-room line (the passing sides); 41.4" from the bottom edge to the bottom wall and 32.75" to the buffet's face, which floats above the chair backs (tight:32.8: the chairs push back under it, but nobody passes behind them, so the buffet is reached from its ends; it was 27.4" until the final audit 2026-10-26 moved the set 5.4" toward the living room); 32.25" from the right end to the right wall (tight:32.3, Maddie's end). With the leaf in (70 7/8"), seat three to a side and leave both ends empty: nothing stands in the kitchen walkway (F-42), nobody sits at the right end, 20.4" from the wall (tight:20.4), and Maddie's URBAN goes on the living-room side, where a parent can stand behind her. Six placemats fit on the table only with the leaf in.
On bare tile the chairs scrape, so put felt pads under their legs (not in the catalog).
Audit 2026-10-24: the wall lamps moved 1.4" outward (lamp-1 to x 39.1, lamp-2 to x 98.2), each now centered between the canvas and the buffet's end with 2.4" on either side; at 1" from the frame they crowded the canvas. Guest evenings seat three to a side, where the old plan put the kitchen-end chair 11.8" into the walkway. F-36 now gives the buffet's face as well as the wall, and the HAVSRIS cord drop goes in a cord cover, never across an open side or down the wall behind Maddie's seat.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the set moved 5.4" toward the living room (the table's top edge at y 48.5, chairs 1 and 2 backing onto the walkway's edge like the kitchen-end chair), which took the buffet's face from 27.4" to 32.75" off the table and the bottom wall to 41.4"; the rug stays out (F-66). The HAVSRIS cord is 15' 5" (185"): with the ceiling unmeasured, check for a ceiling box first, and measure the ceiling and find the bottom wall's outlets before buying it (its drop and run need about 171" at a 96" ceiling and about 195" at 108", F-23).

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Staged pick, 2026-10-24, re-staged 2026-10-25 (F-64) and 2026-10-26 on Andrew's IKEA cart (the RÅDMANSÖ king with the LURÖY base and a RÅDMANSÖ nightstand by the reading chair; the Mellow room is i): a king and no dresser. Andrew: "there is a GIANT walk in closet ... so we do NOT need a dresser for sure", "Try to fit a kingsize bed in master, still need nightstands", and of the dresser layouts "Keep the dresser version in tact though as alternaitves, it's still nice": they are b to g, g being the MALM queen staged before. On 2026-10-25 he agreed to a 30" landing at the bathroom door ("ok") and measured the heater at 3" deep, so the king moved 5.75" toward the bathroom and its window side took a floor nightstand. 14 placed, 12 extras; the cart's pieces are shortlisted, the rest suggestions (F-50).
A king fits one way only: headboard end on the right (bathroom) wall, between the heater's face (3" off the wall, F-33) and the bathroom door's 30" landing (y 103, F-31): 100" for the frame and the window side together. Andrew's RÅDMANSÖ king (79 1/8", his cart, 2026-10-26) leaves the window side 20 7/8" to its frame (tight:20.9: F-34 wants 24"); the Mellow pine platform (75.5"), the narrowest king in the catalog, gave it 24" and is now layout i. The floor beside the head is the VIHALS's on the window side and the bathroom landing's on the other, so the bathroom side's nightstand is a LACK wall shelf at the same height; the RÅDMANSÖ nightstand in the cart (21 1/4" along the wall, plus F-33's 6" off the heater: 27 1/4") fits neither side.
bed-1 RÅDMANSÖ king with the LURÖY base, brown walnut effect (floor): right wall, rot 90, x 89.625-177, y 23.875-103, its 76" mattress about y 25.4-101.4. F-34: the window side 20 7/8" from the heater's face to the frame (22 3/8" to the mattress) along its whole length, since the heater runs the whole wall (tight:20.9; 24" min, 30" preferred); the bathroom side 30" from the frame to the bottom wall, across the bathroom door's landing (F-31, assumed-position:door). F-33: bedding 22 3/8" from the heater's face (12" min). Its sloped headboard (40 1/2" at the higher setting) stands against the wall under the art; 7 7/8" under the frame.
nightstand-1 VIHALS nightstand, white, 14 5/8" square and 22 7/8" tall (floor): the window side of the head, rot 90, x 162.375-177, y 9-23.625, 6" off the heater's face (F-33: 6" min) and 1/4" off the frame; its 22 7/8" top about level with the mattress's (the frame's rail height isn't on IKEA's page). The getting-in side runs from it to the foot, 72.75" long and 20 7/8" wide (tight:20.9, F-34). shelf-1 LACK wall shelf, white (top): the bathroom side of the head, x 166.75-177, y 104-115.75, 1" off the frame, mounted with its top at 22 7/8", level with the VIHALS. It takes no floor, though its outer 2.25" (x 166.75-169) hangs over the bathroom landing's corner at knee height (F-31). Anchored for 6.5 lb (IKEA), with corner guards, since Maddie will pull on it: a phone, a cup, a book. night-light-1 TÖVÄDER battery night light (top) on it, x 168-171, y 107-113, beside the bathroom door, on its 8-hour timer: the glow for the night walk to the bathroom, about 18' around the bench for the window-side sleeper, with no cord. Two different whites at one height under matching lamps read as a pair.
lamp-1, lamp-2 VARPTROSS bamboo wall lamps (top): on the head wall, mirrored about the bed's center line, x 172-177, y 16.9-23.9 (over the VIHALS) and 103-110 (over the shelf), hung about 24" above the mattress, beside the pillows. Their light is a soft glow (5.9 W): bedside light, not a reading light; a swing-arm reading lamp for each side is still to find. Plug-in on a 7' 3" cord: about 45" goes down to the floor, leaving about 40" along the head wall in a cord cover, so each needs an outlet within about 40" of it, or a short extension in the cover (where the outlets are is still open), never run down past the heater. art-1 PJÄTTERYD endless tide 55x22" (top): right wall over the bed, centered, x 176-177, y 35.9-90.9: 70% of the frame's width, 12" from each lamp; its bottom edge about 50" off the floor, 9 1/2" above the RÅDMANSÖ's 40 1/2" headboard, on two closed security hangers into anchors, since Maddie climbs on the bed (F-21); confirm on its page that the print has no glass.
rug-1 TIOKRONA 7'10"x9'10" jute (rug): rot 0, x 40-158, y 17-111, centered on the bed's width: under its lower 68.4" (78%), 7.4" out on each side and 49.6" past the foot, to 7.75" short of the reading chair; 14" off the heater's face (F-33) and 22" short of the bottom wall. That is all this room gives a king's rug: F-33 caps the window side at 12" off the heater's face, and turned crosswise its 118" would run from the heater's 12" to the bottom wall, so its edges fall where the feet land (designers want 18" to 24" past each side). For this room the rug-free H is the stronger plan. On two STOPP FILT underlays (extras), as its page asks.
bench-1 PERJOHAN pine bench (floor): across the foot, rot 90, x 76.625-87.625, y 43.75-83.1, centered on the bed and 2" off its foot, so the duvet hangs free: pine with the pine frame, a seat and blanket storage.
chair-1 POÄNG (floor): the reading corner where the dresser stood, left wall, rot 270, x 0-32.25, y 21-47.75, facing the bed. F-33: upholstery 18" from the heater's face (12" min). floor-lamp-1 TÅGARP (floor): beside it in the window-side corner, x 0-9, y 12-21; F-33: 9" from the heater's face; its 3' 3" cord along the left wall behind the POÄNG, never to the heater wall: it reaches an outlet within about 24" of baseboard behind the chair (y under about 40), else a flat extension along the baseboard behind the chair and the side table, never across the floor (where the outlets are is still open). nightstand-2 RÅDMANSÖ nightstand, brown walnut effect, 21 1/4x15 1/8x22 7/8" (floor): one of the two in Andrew's cart, at the chair's right arm in place of the LACK side table, left wall, rot 270, x 0-15.125, y 48.75-70, its drawer and shelf facing the room for a book and a cup, its cable slot for a phone charger (where the outlets are is still open); plant-1 FEJKA areca palm on it (top), x 1.5-6.25, y 64-68.75. The second nightstand has no place in this room (above).
storage-1 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors, 23 5/8x16 1/2x76" (floor): left wall between the side table and the door, rot 270, x 0-16.5, y 71.4-95: linens and towels. 1 3/8" below the nightstand, 8" above the door's swing (F-31, assumed-position:door), 8.7" beside the closet's landing, with the open floor in front of its doors (F-32); anchor-required (F-21: 76"). Until the final audit 2026-10-26 it stood on the bottom wall at x 70-93.6, y 116.5-133, where the walk to the bathroom had to turn between its corner and the king's foot corner: 14" on the diagonal (3.9" by 13.5"), though the notes gave 31.8" before the turn and 30" after it (F-30).
Walkways (F-30): from the door to the bathroom along the bottom of the room, 49.9" of open floor below the bench's lower end, then 30" beside the bed, from the mattress to the bottom wall, the bathroom landing's depth (36" preferred: the 6" went to the window side), with nothing on that wall; 44.4" between the reading chair and the bench (61.5" from the nightstand) up to the window side, and 40.75" between the bench's top end and the heater's face; the closet's 36" landing and the bathroom door's 30" stay clear (F-31, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:door).
Extras: the king's VALEVÅG King mattress x1 (ships rolled, F-22), DVALA King sheet set x1, SMÅSPORRE King insert x1, ÄNGSLILJA King cover set, natural x1, VILDKORN Queen pillows x2 (the DVALA King's cases are 20x36, king pillows; none in the catalog), FJÄDRAR inner cushions x2 in VALLKRASSING gray-green covers x2, VALLKRASSING throw x1, BENGTA block-out panels x2 on the HUGAD rod x1 (about 107", centered on the window; cut the drop to end above the heater, F-33; assumed-position:window), STOPP FILT underlays x2 under the rug, and the PURRPINGLA laundry bag x1 in the walk-in closet, with the clothes, so the bed wall shows the nightstands and the art, not the laundry.
Not placed: a dresser (the walk-in closet holds the clothes; the STORKLINTA is in b to g); a floor nightstand on the bathroom side (the door's landing); the NISSEDAL mirror ikea-30503778 (glass with no safety film on its page, F-21, and on the bottom wall it narrowed the 30" walk to the bathroom to 29": a dressing mirror for inside the walk-in closet, with a safety film, is still to choose); the STORSEGEL table lamps (12" tall: too low to read by; the wall lamps replace them); the Mellow king (i: 3 5/8" narrower, 24" on the window side) and the Yaheetech, TARVA and MALM kings; the LACK side table (the RÅDMANSÖ nightstand took its place); the second RÅDMANSÖ nightstand ikea-80593597 in the cart (above: 27 1/4" needed on the window side, 20 7/8" there; the bathroom side is the landing).
Total $2,217.76: placed $1,368.89 + extras $848.87; prices as of 2026-10-22 to 2026-10-26 (those before today price-stale, F-02). Before the audit of 2026-10-25, $1,985.75 (the mirror and the hamper on the plan, no night light); with two LACK shelves for nightstands, before that day, $1,953.75; the old staged MALM queen with the dresser, now g, is $1,938.79.
Flags across the layout: tight:20.9 (the king's window side, F-34), assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, anchor-required (storage-1). The tight:15.5 of the 36" landing and the 6" heater went on 2026-10-25; the RÅDMANSÖ brought tight:20.9 on 2026-10-26.
Audit 2026-10-24: the king stays on the bathroom wall, the only arrangement every rule allows (on the left wall it would mirror this with its head beside the room door); bamboo wall lamps hang above both sides, the hamper left the entry, and the reading chair gained a side table. 2026-10-25: with the heater measured at 3" and a 30" bathroom landing, the bed moved 5.75" toward the bathroom, its window side went from 15.5" (tight) to 24.25" (24" to the mattress), and the window-side shelf became the VIHALS. That day's audit took the glass mirror off the bathroom path (F-21, F-30), moved the hamper into the walk-in closet, put a battery night light by the bathroom door, stood the bench 2" off the bed, moved the rug 6" toward the head and centered it on the bed, and noted that the wall lamps are a glow, not reading lights. Window stops: the bed's foot end lies beside the window (assumed-position:window), and Maddie climbs. New York City's window-guard rule covers buildings with three or more apartments, not this two-family house, so ask the landlord for window stops on the upstairs windows.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the BESTÅ column moved from the bottom wall to the left wall below the side table, since the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 14" from the king's foot (the 31.8" and 30" above were measured along the walls, not through the turn, F-30); the art hangs higher, on security hangers; the lamp's outlet is named. Later that day Andrew's IKEA cart chose the RÅDMANSÖ king with its LURÖY base over the Mellow, so the slot is re-worked for a 79 1/8" frame: 133 - 3 - 79.125 - 30 = 20.875 on the window side (tight:20.9, F-34; the mattress 22 3/8" from the heater's face, F-33), the 30" landing kept (only Andrew shortens it). The right wall's 133" is derived from the left wall, not measured, and he can't tape it before the move (F-07): the king is placed by the tape on move-in day, before its boxes are opened. At 133" or more it goes in as drawn; down to 127" it slides toward the heater by the shortfall, the VIHALS comes off the window side (it needs 6" off the heater's face; a second LACK shelf takes its place) and the window side narrows to 14 7/8" at 127", a squeeze whose sleeper gets in from the foot; under 127" the RÅDMANSÖ and the VALEVÅG go back unopened and g, the MALM queen (63", 13" of slack), is the plan. The VALEVÅG King is not in the cart yet.

A plain frame with nothing to pull out; its page offers MALM underbed boxes (b).
nightstand-1 VIHALS white (floor): window side of the head, x 162.375-177, y 16.25-30.875. F-33: 13.25" from the heater face (6" min). White on both sides of the dark bed, like the dresser and the BESTÅ column. shelf-1 LACK wall shelf, white, 11 3/4x10 1/4" (top): the bathroom side's nightstand, hung at mattress height, x 166.75-177, y 97-108.75, so the floor in front of the bathroom door (eyeballed at x 139-169) stays clear for the full 36" (F-31, assumed-position:door). 6.5 lb max and anchored (IKEA): the lamp, a phone and a book.
lamp-1, lamp-2 VARPTROSS bamboo wall lamps (top): on the head wall, one over the nightstand and one over the shelf, about 24" above the mattress, their cords in covers along the head wall to an outlet (where the outlets are is still open); they replace the 12" STORSEGEL table lamps, too low to read by (final audit 2026-10-26). art-1 PJÄTTERYD endless tide 55x22" (top): right wall over the 39 3/8" headboard, centered on the bed, x 176-177, y 36.4-91.4.
rug-1 TIOKRONA 7'10"x9'10" jute (rug): rot 0, x 40-158, y 18-112: under the lower 64" of the bed, 12.9" past its window side, 15" past its bathroom side and 53.9" past the foot; it stops short of the nightstand so it stands level, 15" off the heater face (F-33) and 21" short of the bottom wall.
dresser-1 STORKLINTA 6-drawer (floor): left wall facing the bed, x 0-18.9, y 30-85.1. F-32: 75" from its drawer fronts to the bed's foot; F-31: 17.9" clear of the door's swing (assumed-position:door); anchor-required (F-21, 29.5"; anchor/unlock drawers). plant-1 FEJKA areca palm (top): on its window end.
floor-lamp-1 TÅGARP (floor): top-left corner, x 0-9, y 12-21. F-33: 9" from the heater face.
hamper-1 PURRPINGLA (floor): left wall by the door, x 0-17.3, y 92-100. F-31: 3" clear of the door's swing (assumed-position:door).
Walkways (F-30): door to bathroom along the bottom of the room, 36" beside the bed and open floor before it (the BESTÅ column came off the bottom wall at the final audit 2026-10-26: the walk had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot); the 36" landings in front of the closet and the bathroom door stay clear (F-31, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:door).
Window wall: nothing but the curtains and a corner lamp. F-33 allows furniture 6" off the heater, but a cabinet there would box in the heater along the whole hatched wall, so no BESTÅ goes on it.
Extras: STOPP FILT underlays x2 under the rug, VALEVÅG mattress x1, DVALA queen sheet set x1, SMÅSPORRE Full/Queen insert x1, ÄNGSLILJA natural cover set x1, VILDKORN pillows x2, FJÄDRAR inner cushions x2 in VALLKRASSING gray-green covers x2, VALLKRASSING throw x1, BENGTA block-out panels x2 on the HUGAD rod x1 (about 107", centered on the window; cut the drop to end above the heater, F-33; assumed-position:window).
Not placed: the BESTÅ column ikea-39429699 (on the bottom wall the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot, F-30, final audit 2026-10-26; the walk-in closet and the dresser hold the linens, and f keeps it on the left wall); the NISSEDAL mirror ikea-30503778 (glass with no safety film on its page, F-21; a dressing mirror with one goes inside the walk-in closet); the STORSEGEL table lamps ikea-60573576 (12" tall, too low to read by; the wall lamps replace them); the MALM King ikea-09574348, the bed Andrew linked (82 1/4" wide: under 24" on each side, F-34); the PERJOHAN bench ikea-60485339 (pine against the dark veneer; e keeps it with the TARVA); the BRIMNES 3-door wardrobe ikea-90574800 (no wall takes 46" plus 24" in front); the 50 3/4" BESTÅ ikea-69429706 (rejected: the 76" one stores more on the same footprint); the STORKLINTA and HEMNES 2-drawer nightstands ikea-90559296 and ikea-80242627 (18 7/8" and 15" deep, they stood in front of the bathroom doorway; design review 2026-10-23) and a second VIHALS nightstand (any floor nightstand on the bathroom side stands in the bathroom door's 36" landing, F-31); the black BRIMNES bed ikea-79129608 (its white twin is in f).
Why every variant keeps the headboard on the right wall: the top wall is the heater wall (F-33); on the left wall a queen plus two nightstands needs 102" and only 91" is free between the heater clearance (y 12) and the door's swing (y 103); on the 70" bottom wall the nightstands would stand in the closet's and the bathroom door's 36" landings.
Total $1,718.78: placed $929.90 + extras $788.88; prices as of 2026-10-22 (price-stale, F-02) except the BESTÅ pieces, read 2026-10-23. Flags across the layout: assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, anchor-required (dresser-1).
Re-staged 2026-10-23 (F-64): the MALM in place of the TARVA. How the bathroom side came to a floating shelf is in e's notes.
2026-10-25: the heater is 3" deep (Andrew), not the 6" drawn when this layout was made, and the clearances to its face above are restated to the 3" (final audit 2026-10-26); the bathroom door's landing is now 30" (Andrew: "ok"), and this layout keeps 36" clear in front of it.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the BESTÅ column came off the bottom wall, where the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot (F-30; 36" beside the bed was measured along the wall, not through the turn); the glass NISSEDAL came off the plan (F-21: no safety film on its page); the STORSEGEL table lamps became VARPTROSS wall lamps over the nightstand and the shelf; the TIOKRONA got its two STOPP FILT underlays, as its page asks.

</details>

2026-10-25: the heater is 3" deep (Andrew), not the 6" drawn when this layout was made, and the clearances to its face above are restated to the 3" (final audit 2026-10-26); the bathroom door's landing is now 30" (Andrew: "ok"), and this layout keeps 36" clear in front of it.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the BESTÅ column came off the bottom wall, where the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot (F-30; 36" beside the bed was measured along the wall, not through the turn); the glass NISSEDAL came off the plan (F-21: no safety film on its page); the STORSEGEL table lamps became VARPTROSS wall lamps over the nightstand and the shelf; the TIOKRONA got its two STOPP FILT underlays, as its page asks; the rug moved 4" left, clear of the nightstand.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Alternative: the TARVA layout that was the staged pick until 2026-10-23, when Andrew chose a simpler MALM for the bed (a, F-64). Re-reviewed 2026-10-23 for F-63. Targeted changes, not a re-plan: the bed, nightstands, dresser and bench were right and stay; the 70" bottom wall gets the one storage piece it can take, the mirror moves onto the plan, and the rug moves off the bathroom walkway so the new column stands on the floor. 12 placed, 11 extras; every piece a suggestion in a staged layout (F-50).
bed-1 TARVA queen (floor): right wall, headboard centered, rot 90, x 94.75-177, y 34-97. F-34: 31" on the window side to the heater's face, 36" on the bathroom side (24" min, 30" preferred), the edge of the bathroom door's 36" landing (F-31); F-33: bedding 31" from the heater face.
nightstand-1 IKEA PS 2026 pine (floor): window side of the head, x 165.25-177, y 22.25-34. F-33: 19.25" from the heater face. Pine, like the TARVA bed and the PERJOHAN bench. shelf-1 LACK wall shelf, white, 11 3/4x10 1/4" (top): the bathroom side's nightstand, hung at mattress height, x 166.75-177, y 97-108.75, so the floor in front of the bathroom door (eyeballed at x 139-169) stays clear for the full 36" (F-31, assumed-position:door). 6.5 lb max and anchored (IKEA): the lamp, a phone and a book.
lamp-1, lamp-2 VARPTROSS bamboo wall lamps (top): on the head wall, one over the nightstand and one over the shelf, about 24" above the mattress, their cords in covers along the head wall to an outlet (where the outlets are is still open); they replace the 12" STORSEGEL table lamps, too low to read by (final audit 2026-10-26). art-1 PJÄTTERYD endless tide 55x22" (top): right wall over the headboard, centered, x 176-177, y 38-93.
rug-1 TIOKRONA 7'10"x9'10" jute (rug): rot 0, x 40-158, y 18.5-112.5: under the lower 63" of the bed and the bench, 15.5" past each side and 54.75" past the foot; it stops short of the nightstand so it stands level, 15.5" off the heater face (F-33) and 20.5" short of the bottom wall.
dresser-1 STORKLINTA 6-drawer (floor): left wall facing the bed, x 0-18.9, y 30-85.1. F-32: 62.9" from its drawer fronts to the bench; F-31: 17.9" clear of the door's swing (assumed-position:door); anchor-required (F-21, 29.5"; anchor/unlock drawers). plant-1 FEJKA areca palm (top): on its window end.
bench-1 PERJOHAN pine (floor): across the foot of the bed, 2" off it, x 81.75-92.75, y 45.8-85.2. F-30: 62.9" to the dresser, 47.8" to the bottom wall.
floor-lamp-1 TÅGARP (floor): top-left corner, x 0-9, y 12-21. F-33: 9" from the heater face.
hamper-1 PURRPINGLA (floor): left wall by the door, x 0-17.3, y 92-100. F-31: 3" clear of the door's swing (assumed-position:door).
Walkways (F-30): door to bathroom along the bottom of the room, 36" beside the bed and open floor before it (the BESTÅ column came off the bottom wall at the final audit 2026-10-26: the walk had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot); the 36" landings in front of the closet and the bathroom door stay clear (F-31, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:door).
Window wall: nothing but the curtains and a corner lamp. F-33 allows furniture 6" off the heater, but a cabinet there would box in the heater along the whole hatched wall, so no BESTÅ goes on it.
Extras: STOPP FILT underlays x2 under the rug, VALEVÅG mattress x1, DVALA queen sheet set x1, SMÅSPORRE Full/Queen insert x1, ÄNGSLILJA natural cover set x1, VILDKORN pillows x2, FJÄDRAR inner cushions x2 in VALLKRASSING gray-green covers x2, VALLKRASSING throw x1, BENGTA block-out panels x2 on the HUGAD rod x1 (about 107", centered on the window; cut the drop to end above the heater, F-33; assumed-position:window).
Not placed: the BESTÅ column ikea-39429699 (on the bottom wall the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot, F-30, final audit 2026-10-26; the walk-in closet and the dresser hold the linens, and f keeps it on the left wall); the NISSEDAL mirror ikea-30503778 (glass with no safety film on its page, F-21; a dressing mirror with one goes inside the walk-in closet); the STORSEGEL table lamps ikea-60573576 (12" tall, too low to read by; the wall lamps replace them); the BRIMNES 3-door wardrobe ikea-90574800 (no wall takes 46" plus 24" in front); the 50 3/4" BESTÅ ikea-69429706 (rejected: the 76" one stores more on the same footprint); the STORKLINTA and HEMNES 2-drawer nightstands ikea-90559296 and ikea-80242627 (18 7/8" and 15" deep, they stood in front of the bathroom doorway; design review 2026-10-23) and a second IKEA PS 2026 nightstand (any floor nightstand on the bathroom side stands in the bathroom door's 36" landing, F-31); the black BRIMNES bed ikea-79129608 (its white twin is in f).
Why every variant keeps the headboard on the right wall: the top wall is the heater wall (F-33); on the left wall a queen plus two nightstands needs 102" and only 91" is free between the heater clearance (y 12) and the door's swing (y 103); on the 70" bottom wall the nightstands would stand in the closet's and the bathroom door's 36" landings.

With F-31's 36" landing in front of every door, the bathroom side takes the LACK floating shelf and the bed group moves up 1", so the bed's side is 36" from the bottom wall.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Staged until 2026-10-24, when the king took a (F-64); kept as the dresser alternative (Andrew: "Keep the dresser version in tact though as alternaitves, it's still nice"). Its arrangement is unchanged.
Staged pick, re-staged 2026-10-23 for Andrew's bed (F-64): "For master bed, I prefer simpler beds like [the MALM] (no drawers), but have drawers available in case wife says so." His link is the MALM King, too wide for this room (ikea-09574348); this is the same bed in Queen, dark-brown veneer, bought in the store (Last chance: 2 of 3 at IKEA Long Island, no delivery). The bedside goes white to sit with the dark wood, the white dresser and the BESTÅ column, and the pine bench at the foot comes off: pine against the dark veneer, and the floor stays open. b is this layout with the MALM's drawers; e keeps the TARVA layout staged before. 11 placed, 11 extras; every piece a suggestion in a staged layout (F-50).
bed-1 MALM queen, dark-brown veneer/Luröy (floor): right wall, between the heater clearance and the bathroom door's landing, rot 90, x 93.875-177, y 30.875-97. F-34: 27.9" on the window side to the heater's face (24" min, 30" preferred: the MALM is 66 1/8" wide, 3" wider than the TARVA), 36" on the bathroom side, the edge of the bathroom door's 36" landing (F-31); F-33: bedding 27.9" from the heater face. A plain frame with nothing to pull out; its page offers MALM underbed boxes (b).
nightstand-1 VIHALS white (floor): window side of the head, x 162.375-177, y 16.25-30.875. F-33: 13.25" from the heater face (6" min). White on both sides of the dark bed, like the dresser and the BESTÅ column. shelf-1 LACK wall shelf, white, 11 3/4x10 1/4" (top): the bathroom side's nightstand, hung at mattress height, x 166.75-177, y 97-108.75, so the floor in front of the bathroom door (eyeballed at x 139-169) stays clear for the full 36" (F-31, assumed-position:door). 6.5 lb max and anchored (IKEA): the lamp, a phone and a book.
lamp-1, lamp-2 VARPTROSS bamboo wall lamps (top): on the head wall, one over the nightstand and one over the shelf, about 24" above the mattress, their cords in covers along the head wall to an outlet (where the outlets are is still open); they replace the 12" STORSEGEL table lamps, too low to read by (final audit 2026-10-26). art-1 PJÄTTERYD endless tide 55x22" (top): right wall over the 39 3/8" headboard, centered on the bed, x 176-177, y 36.4-91.4.
rug-1 TIOKRONA 7'10"x9'10" jute (rug): rot 0, x 40-158, y 18-112: under the lower 64" of the bed, 12.9" past its window side, 15" past its bathroom side and 53.9" past the foot; it stops short of the nightstand so it stands level, 15" off the heater face (F-33) and 21" short of the bottom wall.
dresser-1 STORKLINTA 6-drawer (floor): left wall facing the bed, x 0-18.9, y 30-85.1. F-32: 75" from its drawer fronts to the bed's foot; F-31: 17.9" clear of the door's swing (assumed-position:door); anchor-required (F-21, 29.5"; anchor/unlock drawers). plant-1 FEJKA areca palm (top): on its window end.
floor-lamp-1 TÅGARP (floor): top-left corner, x 0-9, y 12-21. F-33: 9" from the heater face.
hamper-1 PURRPINGLA (floor): left wall by the door, x 0-17.3, y 92-100. F-31: 3" clear of the door's swing (assumed-position:door).
Walkways (F-30): door to bathroom along the bottom of the room, 36" beside the bed and open floor before it (the BESTÅ column came off the bottom wall at the final audit 2026-10-26: the walk had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot); the 36" landings in front of the closet and the bathroom door stay clear (F-31, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:door).
Window wall: nothing but the curtains and a corner lamp. F-33 allows furniture 6" off the heater, but a cabinet there would box in the heater along the whole hatched wall, so no BESTÅ goes on it.
Extras: STOPP FILT underlays x2 under the rug, VALEVÅG mattress x1, DVALA queen sheet set x1, SMÅSPORRE Full/Queen insert x1, ÄNGSLILJA natural cover set x1, VILDKORN pillows x2, FJÄDRAR inner cushions x2 in VALLKRASSING gray-green covers x2, VALLKRASSING throw x1, BENGTA block-out panels x2 on the HUGAD rod x1 (about 107", centered on the window; cut the drop to end above the heater, F-33; assumed-position:window).
Not placed: the BESTÅ column ikea-39429699 (on the bottom wall the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot, F-30, final audit 2026-10-26; the walk-in closet and the dresser hold the linens, and f keeps it on the left wall); the NISSEDAL mirror ikea-30503778 (glass with no safety film on its page, F-21; a dressing mirror with one goes inside the walk-in closet); the STORSEGEL table lamps ikea-60573576 (12" tall, too low to read by; the wall lamps replace them); the MALM King ikea-09574348, the bed Andrew linked (82 1/4" wide: under 24" on each side, F-34); the PERJOHAN bench ikea-60485339 (pine against the dark veneer; e keeps it with the TARVA); the BRIMNES 3-door wardrobe ikea-90574800 (no wall takes 46" plus 24" in front); the 50 3/4" BESTÅ ikea-69429706 (rejected: the 76" one stores more on the same footprint); the STORKLINTA and HEMNES 2-drawer nightstands ikea-90559296 and ikea-80242627 (18 7/8" and 15" deep, they stood in front of the bathroom doorway; design review 2026-10-23) and a second VIHALS nightstand (any floor nightstand on the bathroom side stands in the bathroom door's 36" landing, F-31); the black BRIMNES bed ikea-79129608 (its white twin is in f).
Why every variant keeps the headboard on the right wall: the top wall is the heater wall (F-33); on the left wall a queen plus two nightstands needs 102" and only 91" is free between the heater clearance (y 12) and the door's swing (y 103); on the 70" bottom wall the nightstands would stand in the closet's and the bathroom door's 36" landings.
Total $1,618.78: placed $929.90 + extras $688.88; prices as of 2026-10-22 (price-stale, F-02) except the BESTÅ pieces, read 2026-10-23. Flags across the layout: assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, anchor-required (dresser-1).
Re-staged 2026-10-23 (F-64): the MALM in place of the TARVA. How the bathroom side came to a floating shelf is in e's notes.
2026-10-25: the heater is 3" deep (Andrew), not the 6" drawn when this layout was made, and the clearances to its face above are restated to the 3" (final audit 2026-10-26); the bathroom door's landing is now 30" (Andrew: "ok"), and this layout keeps 36" clear in front of it.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the BESTÅ column came off the bottom wall, where the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 19.5" from the bed's foot (F-30; 36" beside the bed was measured along the wall, not through the turn); the glass NISSEDAL came off the plan (F-21: no safety film on its page); the STORSEGEL table lamps became VARPTROSS wall lamps over the nightstand and the shelf; the TIOKRONA got its two STOPP FILT underlays, as its page asks.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Rug-free (F-66), 2026-10-24, re-staged 2026-10-25 and 2026-10-26 with a (Andrew's cart: the RÅDMANSÖ king and nightstand): the staged king layout with no rug, for dust and dust mites. Andrew: "keep the rug / carpet version. But my wife is suggesting we don't use carpets so we don't worry about dust / mites as much." 13 placed, 11 extras. In this room it is the stronger plan: a rug under the king can reach only 9.25" past each side (a, rug-1).
What replaced the TIOKRONA: the bed is the anchor, framed by the white VIHALS and the white floating shelf at one height under matching bamboo wall lamps (light at the bed, F-66 2), and the PJÄTTERYD over it, 55" over 79 1/8", 70%, near the two thirds F-66 asks for (3); the pine bench across its foot and the walnut-toned frame carry the jute's warm tone, with the bamboo of the lamps, and the palette moves up off the floor into the bedding and textiles (4): the natural ÄNGSLILJA cover, the VALLKRASSING gray-green throw and cushion covers, the BENGTA panels. Machine-washable by their pages: the VALLKRASSING covers (warm), the FJÄDRAR inserts (warm, to 60°C) and the SMÅSPORRE King insert (60°C, which its page says eliminates dust mites); the VILDKORN pillows "can be washed frequently at high temperatures"; the ÄNGSLILJA, DVALA, BENGTA and the throw need their care labels read. The rug's second zone, the floor out to the reading chair, keeps its own light, the TÅGARP, with the side table and the plant (5). Softness underfoot is the one job nothing replaces: slippers by the bed. With the floor bare, the whole room mops in one pass.
A king fits one way only: headboard end on the right (bathroom) wall, between the heater's face (3" off the wall, F-33) and the bathroom door's 30" landing (y 103, F-31): 100" for the frame and the window side together. Andrew's RÅDMANSÖ king (79 1/8", his cart, 2026-10-26) leaves the window side 20 7/8" to its frame (tight:20.9: F-34 wants 24"); the Mellow pine platform (75.5"), the narrowest king in the catalog, gave it 24" and is now layout i. The floor beside the head is the VIHALS's on the window side and the bathroom landing's on the other, so the bathroom side's nightstand is a LACK wall shelf at the same height; the RÅDMANSÖ nightstand in the cart (21 1/4" along the wall, plus F-33's 6" off the heater: 27 1/4") fits neither side.
bed-1 RÅDMANSÖ king with the LURÖY base, brown walnut effect (floor): right wall, rot 90, x 89.625-177, y 23.875-103, its 76" mattress about y 25.4-101.4. F-34: the window side 20 7/8" from the heater's face to the frame (22 3/8" to the mattress) along its whole length, since the heater runs the whole wall (tight:20.9; 24" min, 30" preferred); the bathroom side 30" from the frame to the bottom wall, across the bathroom door's landing (F-31, assumed-position:door). F-33: bedding 22 3/8" from the heater's face (12" min). Its sloped headboard (40 1/2" at the higher setting) stands against the wall under the art; 7 7/8" under the frame.
nightstand-1 VIHALS nightstand, white, 14 5/8" square and 22 7/8" tall (floor): the window side of the head, rot 90, x 162.375-177, y 9-23.625, 6" off the heater's face (F-33: 6" min) and 1/4" off the frame; its 22 7/8" top about level with the mattress's (the frame's rail height isn't on IKEA's page). The getting-in side runs from it to the foot, 72.75" long and 20 7/8" wide (tight:20.9, F-34). shelf-1 LACK wall shelf, white (top): the bathroom side of the head, x 166.75-177, y 104-115.75, 1" off the frame, mounted with its top at 22 7/8", level with the VIHALS. It takes no floor, though its outer 2.25" (x 166.75-169) hangs over the bathroom landing's corner at knee height (F-31). Anchored for 6.5 lb (IKEA), with corner guards, since Maddie will pull on it: a phone, a cup, a book. night-light-1 TÖVÄDER battery night light (top) on it, x 168-171, y 107-113, beside the bathroom door, on its 8-hour timer: the glow for the night walk to the bathroom, about 18' around the bench for the window-side sleeper, with no cord. Two different whites at one height under matching lamps read as a pair.
lamp-1, lamp-2 VARPTROSS bamboo wall lamps (top): on the head wall, mirrored about the bed's center line, x 172-177, y 16.9-23.9 (over the VIHALS) and 103-110 (over the shelf), hung about 24" above the mattress, beside the pillows. Their light is a soft glow (5.9 W): bedside light, not a reading light; a swing-arm reading lamp for each side is still to find. Plug-in on a 7' 3" cord: about 45" goes down to the floor, leaving about 40" along the head wall in a cord cover, so each needs an outlet within about 40" of it, or a short extension in the cover (where the outlets are is still open), never run down past the heater. art-1 PJÄTTERYD endless tide 55x22" (top): right wall over the bed, centered, x 176-177, y 35.9-90.9: 70% of the frame's width, 12" from each lamp; its bottom edge about 50" off the floor, 9 1/2" above the RÅDMANSÖ's 40 1/2" headboard, on two closed security hangers into anchors, since Maddie climbs on the bed (F-21); confirm on its page that the print has no glass.
bench-1 PERJOHAN pine bench (floor): across the foot, rot 90, x 76.625-87.625, y 43.75-83.1, centered on the bed and 2" off its foot, so the duvet hangs free: pine with the pine frame, a seat and blanket storage.
chair-1 POÄNG (floor): the reading corner where the dresser stood, left wall, rot 270, x 0-32.25, y 21-47.75, facing the bed. F-33: upholstery 18" from the heater's face (12" min). floor-lamp-1 TÅGARP (floor): beside it in the window-side corner, x 0-9, y 12-21; F-33: 9" from the heater's face; its 3' 3" cord along the left wall behind the POÄNG, never to the heater wall: it reaches an outlet within about 24" of baseboard behind the chair (y under about 40), else a flat extension along the baseboard behind the chair and the side table, never across the floor (where the outlets are is still open). nightstand-2 RÅDMANSÖ nightstand, brown walnut effect, 21 1/4x15 1/8x22 7/8" (floor): one of the two in Andrew's cart, at the chair's right arm in place of the LACK side table, left wall, rot 270, x 0-15.125, y 48.75-70, its drawer and shelf facing the room for a book and a cup, its cable slot for a phone charger (where the outlets are is still open); plant-1 FEJKA areca palm on it (top), x 1.5-6.25, y 64-68.75. The second nightstand has no place in this room (above).
storage-1 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors, 23 5/8x16 1/2x76" (floor): left wall between the side table and the door, rot 270, x 0-16.5, y 71.4-95: linens and towels. 1 3/8" below the nightstand, 8" above the door's swing (F-31, assumed-position:door), 8.7" beside the closet's landing, with the open floor in front of its doors (F-32); anchor-required (F-21: 76"). Until the final audit 2026-10-26 it stood on the bottom wall at x 70-93.6, y 116.5-133, where the walk to the bathroom had to turn between its corner and the king's foot corner: 14" on the diagonal (3.9" by 13.5"), though the notes gave 31.8" before the turn and 30" after it (F-30).
Walkways (F-30): from the door to the bathroom along the bottom of the room, 49.9" of open floor below the bench's lower end, then 30" beside the bed, from the mattress to the bottom wall, the bathroom landing's depth (36" preferred: the 6" went to the window side), with nothing on that wall; 44.4" between the reading chair and the bench (61.5" from the nightstand) up to the window side, and 40.75" between the bench's top end and the heater's face; the closet's 36" landing and the bathroom door's 30" stay clear (F-31, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:door).
Extras: the king's VALEVÅG King mattress x1 (ships rolled, F-22), DVALA King sheet set x1, SMÅSPORRE King insert x1, ÄNGSLILJA King cover set, natural x1, VILDKORN Queen pillows x2 (the DVALA King's cases are 20x36, king pillows; none in the catalog), FJÄDRAR inner cushions x2 in VALLKRASSING gray-green covers x2, VALLKRASSING throw x1, BENGTA block-out panels x2 on the HUGAD rod x1 (about 107", centered on the window; cut the drop to end above the heater, F-33; assumed-position:window), and the PURRPINGLA laundry bag x1 in the walk-in closet, with the clothes.
Not placed: a dresser (the walk-in closet holds the clothes; the STORKLINTA is in b to g); a floor nightstand on the bathroom side (the door's landing); the NISSEDAL mirror ikea-30503778 (glass with no safety film on its page, F-21, and on the bottom wall it narrowed the 30" walk to the bathroom to 29": a dressing mirror for inside the walk-in closet, with a safety film, is still to choose); the STORSEGEL table lamps (12" tall: too low to read by; the wall lamps replace them); the Mellow king (i: 3 5/8" narrower, 24" on the window side) and the Yaheetech, TARVA and MALM kings; the LACK side table (the RÅDMANSÖ nightstand took its place); the second RÅDMANSÖ nightstand ikea-80593597 in the cart (above: 27 1/4" needed on the window side, 20 7/8" there; the bathroom side is the landing). The TIOKRONA and its underlays (F-66: no textile rug).
Total $2,057.79: placed $1,238.90 + extras $818.89, the staged king less the $129.99 TIOKRONA and its $29.98 underlays; prices as of 2026-10-22 to 2026-10-26 (those before today price-stale, F-02). Before the audit of 2026-10-25, $1,825.78; with two LACK shelves, before that day, $1,793.78.
Flags across the layout: tight:20.9 (the king's window side, F-34), assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, anchor-required (storage-1).
Audit 2026-10-24: the same fixes as a. Rug-free check: zone, anchor and color are replaced (above); of the textiles, the POÄNG's Knisa cushion is the one dust holder its page says to vacuum and wipe, not wash. 2026-10-25: re-staged with a for the 3" heater and the 30" bathroom landing, with the same audit fixes (no glass mirror, the hamper in the closet, the night light, the bench off the bed). Window stops: the bed's foot end lies beside the window (assumed-position:window), and Maddie climbs. New York City's window-guard rule covers buildings with three or more apartments, not this two-family house, so ask the landlord for window stops on the upstairs windows.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the BESTÅ column moved from the bottom wall to the left wall below the side table, since the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 14" from the king's foot (the 31.8" and 30" above were measured along the walls, not through the turn, F-30); the art hangs higher, on security hangers; the lamp's outlet is named. Later that day Andrew's IKEA cart chose the RÅDMANSÖ king with its LURÖY base over the Mellow, so the slot is re-worked for a 79 1/8" frame: 133 - 3 - 79.125 - 30 = 20.875 on the window side (tight:20.9, F-34; the mattress 22 3/8" from the heater's face, F-33), the 30" landing kept (only Andrew shortens it). The right wall's 133" is derived from the left wall, not measured, and he can't tape it before the move (F-07): the king is placed by the tape on move-in day, before its boxes are opened. At 133" or more it goes in as drawn; down to 127" it slides toward the heater by the shortfall, the VIHALS comes off the window side (it needs 6" off the heater's face; a second LACK shelf takes its place) and the window side narrows to 14 7/8" at 127", a squeeze whose sleeper gets in from the foot; under 127" the RÅDMANSÖ and the VALEVÅG go back unopened and g, the MALM queen (63", 13" of slack), is the plan. The VALEVÅG King is not in the cart yet.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Alternative since 2026-10-26: the room as staged before Andrew's IKEA cart chose the RÅDMANSÖ king (a); kept because the Mellow is the one king that gives the window side its 24" (F-34).
Staged from 2026-10-24 (re-staged 2026-10-25, F-64) until Andrew's cart on 2026-10-26: a king and no dresser. Andrew: "there is a GIANT walk in closet ... so we do NOT need a dresser for sure", "Try to fit a kingsize bed in master, still need nightstands", and of the dresser layouts "Keep the dresser version in tact though as alternaitves, it's still nice": they are b to g, g being the MALM queen staged before. On 2026-10-25 he agreed to a 30" landing at the bathroom door ("ok") and measured the heater at 3" deep, so the king moved 5.75" toward the bathroom and its window side took a floor nightstand. 14 placed, 12 extras; every piece a suggestion in a staged layout (F-50).
A king fits one way only: headboard end on the right (bathroom) wall, between 24" off the heater's face for getting in (y 27, F-34; the face is 3" off the wall, F-33) and the bathroom door's 30" landing (y 103, F-31): 76", a king mattress exactly. The Mellow pine platform (75.5"; its mattress overhangs it 1/4" a side) is the narrowest king in the catalog and the only one that fits it; the Yaheetech dark-walnut king (amazon-B0DWMSPBMZ, 78.5") would leave 21.5" on the window side, the TARVA King (79 1/8") 20.9" and the MALM King (82 1/4") 17.75" (tight, F-34). The floor beside the head is the nightstand's on the window side and the bathroom landing's on the other, so the bathroom side's nightstand is a LACK wall shelf at the same height.
bed-1 Mellow king platform, natural pine (floor): right wall, rot 90, x 97.5-177, y 27.25-102.75, its mattress y 27-103. F-34: the window side 24" from the heater's face to the mattress (24.25" to the frame) along its whole length, since the heater runs the whole wall (24" min, 30" preferred), and 27" from the wall; the bathroom side 30" from the mattress to the bottom wall, across the bathroom door's landing (F-31, assumed-position:door). F-33: bedding 24" from the heater's face (12" min). No headboard: the pillows rest against the wall under the art.
nightstand-1 VIHALS nightstand, white, 14 5/8" square and 22 7/8" tall (floor): the window side of the head, rot 90, x 162.375-177, y 11.625-26.25, 1" off the frame and 8.625" off the heater's face (F-33: 6" min); its top 1 3/8" above the mattress's 21 1/2". The getting-in side runs from it to the foot, 64.9" long. shelf-1 LACK wall shelf, white (top): the bathroom side of the head, x 166.75-177, y 103.75-115.5, 1" off the frame, mounted with its top at 22 7/8", level with the VIHALS. It takes no floor, though its outer 2.25" (x 166.75-169) hangs over the bathroom landing's corner at knee height (F-31). Anchored for 6.5 lb (IKEA), with corner guards, since Maddie will pull on it: a phone, a cup, a book. night-light-1 TÖVÄDER battery night light (top) on it, x 168-171, y 106.6-112.6, beside the bathroom door, on its 8-hour timer: the glow for the night walk to the bathroom, about 18' around the bench for the window-side sleeper, with no cord. Two different whites at one height under matching lamps read as a pair.
lamp-1, lamp-2 VARPTROSS bamboo wall lamps (top): on the head wall, mirrored about the bed's center line, x 172-177, y 16.9-23.9 (over the VIHALS) and 106.1-113.1 (over the shelf), hung about 24" above the mattress, beside the pillows. Their light is a soft glow (5.9 W): bedside light, not a reading light; a swing-arm reading lamp for each side is still to find. Plug-in on a 7' 3" cord: about 45" goes down to the floor, leaving about 40" along the head wall in a cord cover, so each needs an outlet within about 40" of it, or a short extension in the cover (where the outlets are is still open), never run down past the heater. art-1 PJÄTTERYD endless tide 55x22" (top): right wall over the bed, centered, x 176-177, y 37.5-92.5: 73% of the bed's width, 13.6" from each lamp; with no headboard it acts as one, so its bottom edge hangs about 54" off the floor, 12" above the propped pillows and clear of a reclining reader's head (48" sat below one: final audit 2026-10-26), on two closed security hangers into anchors, since Maddie climbs on the bed (F-21); confirm on its page that the print has no glass.
rug-1 TIOKRONA 7'10"x9'10" jute (rug): rot 0, x 40-158, y 18-112, centered on the bed's width: under its lower 60.5" (76%), 9.25" out on each side and 57.5" past the foot, to 7.75" short of the reading chair; 15" off the heater's face (F-33) and 21" short of the bottom wall. That is all this room gives a king's rug: F-33 caps the window side at 12.25", and turned crosswise its 118" would run from the heater's 12" to the bottom wall, so its edges fall where the feet land (designers want 18" to 24" past each side). For this room the rug-free H is the stronger plan. On two STOPP FILT underlays (extras), as its page asks.
bench-1 PERJOHAN pine bench (floor): across the foot, rot 90, x 84.5-95.5, y 45.3-84.7, centered on the bed and 2" off it, so the duvet hangs free: pine with the pine frame, a seat and blanket storage.
chair-1 POÄNG (floor): the reading corner where the dresser stood, left wall, rot 270, x 0-32.25, y 21-47.75, facing the bed. F-33: upholstery 18" from the heater's face (12" min). floor-lamp-1 TÅGARP (floor): beside it in the window-side corner, x 0-9, y 12-21; F-33: 9" from the heater's face; its 3' 3" cord along the left wall behind the POÄNG, never to the heater wall: it reaches an outlet within about 24" of baseboard behind the chair (y under about 40), else a flat extension along the baseboard behind the chair and the side table, never across the floor (where the outlets are is still open). side-table-1 LACK side table, white stained oak effect (floor): at the chair's right arm, left wall, rot 270, x 0-21.6, y 48.75-70.4, for a cup and a book; plant-1 FEJKA areca palm on it (top), x 1.5-6.25, y 64-68.75.
storage-1 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors, 23 5/8x16 1/2x76" (floor): left wall between the side table and the door, rot 270, x 0-16.5, y 71.4-95: linens and towels. 1" below the side table, 8" above the door's swing (F-31, assumed-position:door), 8.7" beside the closet's landing, with the open floor in front of its doors (F-32); anchor-required (F-21: 76"). Until the final audit 2026-10-26 it stood on the bottom wall at x 70-93.6, y 116.5-133, where the walk to the bathroom had to turn between its corner and the king's foot corner: 14" on the diagonal (3.9" by 13.5"), though the notes gave 31.8" before the turn and 30" after it (F-30).
Walkways (F-30): from the door to the bathroom along the bottom of the room, 48.3" of open floor below the bench's lower end, then 30" beside the bed, from the mattress to the bottom wall, the bathroom landing's depth (36" preferred: the 6" went to the window side), with nothing on that wall; 52.25" between the reading chair and the bench (62.9" from the side table) up to the window side, and 42.3" between the bench's top end and the heater's face; the closet's 36" landing and the bathroom door's 30" stay clear (F-31, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:door).
Extras: the king's VALEVÅG King mattress x1 (ships rolled, F-22), DVALA King sheet set x1, SMÅSPORRE King insert x1, ÄNGSLILJA King cover set, natural x1, VILDKORN Queen pillows x2 (the DVALA King's cases are 20x36, king pillows; none in the catalog), FJÄDRAR inner cushions x2 in VALLKRASSING gray-green covers x2, VALLKRASSING throw x1, BENGTA block-out panels x2 on the HUGAD rod x1 (about 107", centered on the window; cut the drop to end above the heater, F-33; assumed-position:window), STOPP FILT underlays x2 under the rug, and the PURRPINGLA laundry bag x1 in the walk-in closet, with the clothes, so the bed wall shows the nightstands and the art, not the laundry.
Not placed: a dresser (the walk-in closet holds the clothes; the STORKLINTA is in b to g); a floor nightstand on the bathroom side (the door's landing); the NISSEDAL mirror ikea-30503778 (glass with no safety film on its page, F-21, and on the bottom wall it narrowed the 30" walk to the bathroom to 29": a dressing mirror for inside the walk-in closet, with a safety film, is still to choose); the STORSEGEL table lamps (12" tall: too low to read by; the wall lamps replace them); the Yaheetech, TARVA and MALM kings (each leaves the window side under 24", above).
Total $1,905.75: placed $1,056.88 + extras $848.87; prices as of 2026-10-22 to 2026-10-24, all of them stale today (price-stale, F-02). Before the audit of 2026-10-25, $1,985.75 (the mirror and the hamper on the plan, no night light); with two LACK shelves for nightstands, before that day, $1,953.75; the old staged MALM queen with the dresser, now g, is $1,938.79.
Flags across the layout: assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, anchor-required (storage-1). The tight:15.5 on the window side and heater-depth-assumed are gone (2026-10-25).
Audit 2026-10-24: the king stays on the bathroom wall, the only arrangement every rule allows (on the left wall it would mirror this with its head beside the room door); bamboo wall lamps hang above both sides, the hamper left the entry, and the reading chair gained a side table. 2026-10-25: with the heater measured at 3" and a 30" bathroom landing, the bed moved 5.75" toward the bathroom, its window side went from 15.5" (tight) to 24.25" (24" to the mattress), and the window-side shelf became the VIHALS. That day's audit took the glass mirror off the bathroom path (F-21, F-30), moved the hamper into the walk-in closet, put a battery night light by the bathroom door, stood the bench 2" off the bed, moved the rug 6" toward the head and centered it on the bed, and noted that the wall lamps are a glow, not reading lights. Window stops: the bed's foot end lies beside the window (assumed-position:window), and Maddie climbs. New York City's window-guard rule covers buildings with three or more apartments, not this two-family house, so ask the landlord for window stops on the upstairs windows.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the BESTÅ column moved from the bottom wall to the left wall below the side table, since the walk to the bathroom had to turn its corner 14" from the king's foot (the 31.8" and 30" above were measured along the walls, not through the turn, F-30); the art hangs higher, on security hangers; the lamp's outlet is named. The king's slot has no slack (133 - 3 - 24 - 76 - 30 = 0), the right wall's 133" is derived from the left wall, not measured, and Andrew can't tape it before the move (2026-10-26: "I don't have any other info than what I gave you so far and the photos. Do your best.", F-07): the king is ordered on the measured 133" and placed by the tape on move-in day, before its boxes are opened. At 133" or more it goes in as drawn; down to 131" it slides toward the heater by the shortfall (the window side 22" at least, the VIHALS still 6 5/8" off the heater's face, F-33); down to 129" the bathroom walk takes the rest (28" at least, walkway-narrow); under 129" the Mellow and the VALEVÅG go back unopened and g, the MALM queen (63", 13" of slack), is the plan.
