<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Staged pick (F-41), redesigned 2026-10-23 for F-62 and F-63. The usable floor is a 106 x 106 square: furniture from y 12 and upholstery from y 18 off the hatched heater wall (F-33, heater-depth-assumed), nothing below y 116 in front of the wardrobe or below y 122 in front of the door (F-31: the 36" landings, which hold the swings).
desk-1 MITTZON 55 1/8x31 1/2 white, electric sit/stand: right wall, rot 90, x 74.5-106, y 13.6-68.75; F-33 7.6" from the heater face; F-35 47" from its front to the work desk, 58" to the BESTÅ · fits.
desk-2 MITTZON 47 1/4x31 1/2: right wall below it, y 68.75-116; F-31 6" above the door's landing and on the edge of the wardrobe's (assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet); F-35 41.4" to the chair bed, where the way in passes behind the desk, so the 42" figure applies (tight:41.4; at chair-1 itself 47" and 58"). The personal run is 102 3/8" (F-62): four 32" screens, two per desk on HUANUO arms, the middle pair flat and the outer pair angled in about 38 degrees, so the array spans about 101". Flat they would need 113", and no wall here has more than 110"; the run starts 1 5/8" below the heater line so it ends at the wardrobe's 36" landing, 6" short of the door's, and leaves the chair bed's foot room under desk-2.
desk-3 TRAVSPORT 47 1/4x27 1/2 oak effect, electric sit/stand: left wall, rot 270, x 0-27.5, y 12-59.25: the separate work station, the job laptop on the ELLOVEN beside one 32" monitor with 3/4" to spare (47.25 − 28 − 18.5; a single arm for the monitor above the laptop would free 19", not in the catalog; final audit 2026-10-26); F-33 6" from the heater face; F-35 47" from its front to desk-1 · fits. Both desk walls run perpendicular to the window, so no screen faces it or has it behind.
chair-1 FLINTAN: the personal run's center, x 46-74, y 50.8-78.8, on the bamboo mat · fits. chair-2 FLINTAN: the work desk, x 28-56, y 21.6-49.6; F-33 its seat 15.6" from the heater face · fits. The two chairs sit 1.2" apart in y, so both stations work at once.
storage-2 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors 47 1/4x16 1/2x15 (top, F-63): hung on the left wall above the work desk, x 0-16.5, y 12-59.25, its underside about 70" up so it clears the work monitor with the desk at full height (46 1/8"), its top about 85", under any 8' ceiling; two suspension rails; closed storage for paper, cables and the guest's bedding. It replaces the floor BESTÅ (23 5/8x16 1/2x25 1/4) that stood at y 59.25-82.9 until the final audit 2026-10-26, where the chair bed now needs the floor.
chairbed-1 IKEA PS 2026 chair bed: left wall, rot 270, x 0-33.1, y 79-110.5 (until the final audit 2026-10-26 at y 82.9-114.4, its door-side edge 1.6" from desk-2's end, where a T-foot stands); F-31 5.5" above the wardrobe's 36" landing (assumed-position:closet); F-30 a 41.4" walkway between it and desk-2 from the door to both stations. F-62's small daybed: closed 31 1/2 x 33 1/8", a third of the old HEMNES. Opened it runs 79 7/8" to x 79.9: raise desk-2 and its last 5 3/8" slides under it. The MITTZON's legs stand near each end of the desk with T-feet running front to back; the bed now sits 5.5" from desk-2's door end and 10.25" from the seam. Where the feet stand, read 2026-10-26 from the MITTZON underframe's own page (905.394.30, one telescoping frame sold for the 47 1/4", 55 1/8" and 63" tops; Min. width 44 1/2", Max. width 60 1/4", 2 3/4" less than each top): each foot's outer edge is 1 3/8" in from the desk's end, so the bed's foot clears a leg and foot up to 4 1/8" wide on the door side, and no desk leg is that wide; the leg's own width is not printed on the page. The frame runs 23 5/8" to 48 7/8" high, so raised it clears the 7 1/2" mattress, and ships in two boxes 31" and 30 1/2" long (31 lb 9 oz, 39 lb 2 oz). If the floor says otherwise on the day, this and f give up the chair bed as c does (d's bed lands under desk-1 with 11" and 12 5/8" to its ends). F-34 37.6" of floor along its door side · fits.
lamp-1 TÅGARP: bottom-left corner, x 0.5-9.5, y 142-151, left of the wardrobe's landing (assumed-position:closet), the room's uplighter · fits. plant-1 FEJKA monstera: x 1-8.5, y 117-124.5, left of the wardrobe's landing · fits.
rug-1 LOHALS 4'4"x6'5" flatweave (rug layer): x 25-77, y 20-97, the aisle under both chairs, 2.5" under each desk wall (level the desks' feet on its edge). rug-2 Anji Mountain bamboo chair mat 60 x 48" (rug layer): x 27-75, y 20-80, across the aisle under both chairs on top of the rug, 1/2" under each desk's front edge; wipeable and plastic-free; until the final audit 2026-10-26 a KOLON under chair-1 alone, with chair-2 rolling on the jute and the KOLON's edge under its casters.
lamp-2 RÖDFLIK (top): the work desk's window end. lamp-3 RÖDFLIK (top): on the run's window-end front corner, the run's own light (final audit 2026-10-26: the run had none; the chair bed reads by the TÅGARP's arm). art-1 PJÄTTERYD 3-pack (top): left wall, the three 22" canvases in a row 1" apart (68"), y 64-132, 46-68" up, over the chair bed and the plant.
Extras: the two HOPPVALS blinds; two HUANUO arms and two FÖRSÄSONG cable trays on the personal run; the FREIVID mat; the ELLOVEN; the guest's twin duvet cover, insert and pillow; a cushion and the throw on the chair bed.
Not placed: the 2026-10-22 pieces MICKE (not sit/stand, 19 5/8" deep), ALEX and BILLY (no wall left for them), HEMNES (not small, F-62), MARKUS and CENTERHALV (FLINTAN instead); SKÅDIS (above a sit/stand desk the monitor covers it); TERTIAL (no-dimensions).
Total $3,208.11 (final audit 2026-10-26). Prices: the new pieces as of 2026-10-23; the reused ones (FLINTAN, TÅGARP, RÖDFLIK, FEJKA, LOHALS, PJÄTTERYD, HOPPVALS, FÖRSÄSONG, ELLOVEN, bedding and textiles) as of 2026-10-22, price-stale (F-02).
Audit 2026-10-24 (from the rug-free F): the work desk's window end is 12" from the window wall and chair-2 beside it rolls, so Maddie could climb to the window: fit window stops or guards there (not in the catalog; the sill is unmeasured, F-38). Opened, the chair bed's foot goes under desk-2, so nothing stands under that desk (a PC tower goes under desk-1), and desk-2 stays raised and unplugged while a guest sleeps.
Final audit 2026-10-26, for every office layout: the HUANUO arms are C-clamps on the desks' back edges, and every run desk stands flush against its wall, so either the desk stands off the wall by the clamp's depth (not on the listing: measure the arm; at 2" the way in past the L's return becomes 31.8" in e and g, still over 30") or the arms use their grommet mount (the UPLIFT is configured with two grommets). No outlets are mapped: three motors, five screens, a PC and the lamps need a power strip in each cable tray (the MITTZON page offers KOPPLA, not captured) with a slack loop for the desk's 25" of travel, on the left wall or the right wall above the door. The wardrobe's left door, open 90°, stands on its hinge line at x 10, 1/2" from the TÅGARP and 1 1/2" from the plant's pot: fit a 90° stop on that door, and measure the room door's width and the wardrobe's distance from the right corner (the door as drawn, opened flat, lands 2" over the wardrobe's end). The way from the door to the job desk and the window passes the parked chair at the run (or the L) with 13 to 18 1/2" to spare, so the 30" walkway holds with the chairs pushed in.
Final audit 2026-10-26, this layout: the chair bed moved 3.9" toward the window, centered on desk-2's span, so its opened foot passes between the MITTZON's legs (it was 1.6" from the desk's end, where a T-foot stands); the floor BESTÅ gave way to the wall-hung 47 1/4" over the work desk; one 60 x 48 bamboo mat lies across the aisle under both chairs; the run has its own lamp.

</details>

desk-1 and desk-2 MITTZON 55 1/8 + 47 1/4: the personal run on the right wall, rot 90, x 74.5-106, y 12-114.4 (102 3/8"); F-33 6" from the heater face (heater-depth-assumed); F-31 7.6" above the door's swing (assumed-position:door); F-35 34" from their front to the daybed: 30" for the chair is met, and the 42" for someone passing behind applies only to a guest walking to the daybed's head (tight:34 then) · four 32" screens as in a.

The laptop and the work monitor sit at its right end by the corner, with 9 3/8" to spare at its left end (55.875 − 28 − 18.5; final audit 2026-10-26).
chair-1 FLINTAN: the L's inside corner, x 46-74, y 41-69, facing the run or turned to the return · fits.
daybed-1 VIHALS twin daybed frame (no trundle): left wall, rot 270, x 0-40.5, y 38.9-116; F-33 bedding 32.9" from the heater face; F-31 its foot on the edge of the wardrobe's 36" landing (assumed-position:closet); F-34 34" along its open side to the desks · fits. 77 1/8 x 40 1/2" closed and open: a little smaller than HEMNES and with nothing to pull out, but more than twice the chair bed of a and d.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Alternative (F-41): the chair bed becomes a reading corner at the window end of the left wall, in daylight, with the floor lamp beside it and art above; the work desk moves to the door end under a wall-hung BESTÅ.
desk-1 and desk-2: the personal run on the right wall, rot 90, x 74.5-106, y 12-114.4; F-33 6" from the heater face; F-31 7.6" above the door's swing (assumed-position:door); F-35 47" to the work desk and 65" to the lamp's wall at the chair (41.4" for the chair's top 5", opposite the chair bed) · four 32" screens as in a.
chairbed-1 IKEA PS 2026 chair bed: left wall, rot 270, x 0-33.1, y 23-54.5; F-33 upholstery 17" from the heater face (heater-depth-assumed); 23" in front of the window wall, beside the window's left end (assumed-position:window) · fits. Opened it runs to x 79.9: raise desk-1 and its last 5 3/8" slides under it, centered between desk-1's end legs (5" clear of the window-end leg zone, 6 1/2" of the junction); F-34 the getting-in side faces the open aisle (chair-1 rolled away), and the window side keeps 17" from the heater face (F-33).
lamp-1 TÅGARP: left wall, x 0.5-9.5, y 59.5-68.5, 5" past the chair bed, its arm over it · fits.
desk-3 TRAVSPORT: left wall at the door end, rot 270, x 0-27.5, y 68.75-116; F-31 its end on the edge of the wardrobe's 36" landing at y 116 (assumed-position:closet); F-35 47" to desk-2 · fits.
chair-1 FLINTAN: x 46-74, y 49.2-77.2 at the run's center · fits. chair-2 FLINTAN: x 28-56, y 78.4-106.4 at the work desk · fits.
plant-1 FEJKA: x 1-8.5, y 120-127.5, left of the wardrobe's landing · fits.
rug-1 LOHALS (rug layer): x 25-77, y 32-109, from the chair bed's front edge to the work chair (level the desks' feet on its edge). rug-2 KOLON (rug layer): x 35.1-74.5, y 36-83.25 under chair-1. rug-3 Anji Mountain bamboo chair mat 48 x 36" (rug layer): x 27-75, y 76-112 under chair-2 at the work desk, 1/2" under desk-2's front edge and 4" short of the wardrobe's landing (final audit 2026-10-26: chair-2 rolled on the jute with the KOLON's edge under its casters).
storage-2 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors 47 1/4x16 1/2x15 (top, F-63): hung above the work desk, x 0-16.5, y 68.75-116, its underside about 70" up so it clears the work monitor at full height, two suspension rails. art-1 PJÄTTERYD 3-pack (top): two canvases side by side 1" apart over the chair bed (y 16.25-61.25, 42-64" up), the third over the plant beside the wardrobe. lamp-2 RÖDFLIK (top): the work desk's door end. lamp-4 RÖDFLIK (top): the run's window-end front corner, the run's own light (final audit 2026-10-26).
Extras: as a, plus two suspension rails.
Not placed: the floor BESTÅ (with the chair bed's upholstery 12" or more off the heater face, F-33, the left wall has at most 21" between it and the work desk, short of a 23 5/8" cabinet); the VIHALS daybed (b); the 2026-10-22 MICKE, ALEX, BILLY and HEMNES (see a).
Total $3,167.74 (final audit 2026-10-26). Prices: the new pieces as of 2026-10-23; the reused ones (FLINTAN, TÅGARP, RÖDFLIK, FEJKA, LOHALS, PJÄTTERYD, HOPPVALS, FÖRSÄSONG, ELLOVEN, bedding and textiles) as of 2026-10-22, price-stale (F-02).
Final audit 2026-10-26, for every office layout: the HUANUO arms are C-clamps on the desks' back edges, and every run desk stands flush against its wall, so either the desk stands off the wall by the clamp's depth (not on the listing: measure the arm; at 2" the way in past the L's return becomes 31.8" in e and g, still over 30") or the arms use their grommet mount (the UPLIFT is configured with two grommets). No outlets are mapped: three motors, five screens, a PC and the lamps need a power strip in each cable tray (the MITTZON page offers KOPPLA, not captured) with a slack loop for the desk's 25" of travel, on the left wall or the right wall above the door. The wardrobe's left door, open 90°, stands on its hinge line at x 10, 1/2" from the TÅGARP and 1 1/2" from the plant's pot: fit a 90° stop on that door, and measure the room door's width and the wardrobe's distance from the right corner (the door as drawn, opened flat, lands 2" over the wardrobe's end). The way from the door to the job desk and the window passes the parked chair at the run (or the L) with 13 to 18 1/2" to spare, so the 30" walkway holds with the chairs pushed in.

F-38: it starts 36" below the window wall, so nothing stands in front of the window; F-31: its south end on the wardrobe's landing line (y 116); F-35: 44.5" from the main's front to the job desk · fits.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Rug-free (F-66): the staged arrangement (a) with the LOHALS flatweave taken out; every other piece stands where it does in a, plus one desk lamp. rug-2, the Anji Mountain bamboo chair mat (60 x 48", rug layer, x 27-75, y 20-80), lies across the aisle under both chairs in place of the clear plastic KOLON (Andrew, 2026-10-25: "something that isn't literally plastic"): bamboo on a felt back, wipeable and plastic-free (F-66), and it keeps the casters off the floor; it runs 1/2" under each desk's front edge. Until the final audit 2026-10-26 it was the 48 x 42 under chair-1 alone, with chair-2 rolling on the bare floor and the mat's edge under its casters.
What ties the room together instead:
(1) The anchor is the personal run: the two white MITTZONs end to end along the right wall (102 3/8"), with the work desk and the chair bed facing it across the room.
(2) A lamp for each zone the rug's aisle used to join: a RÖDFLIK on the run's window-end front corner and one on desk-2's door end (it sat on the floor BESTÅ until the final audit 2026-10-26), the RÖDFLIK on the work desk, and the TÅGARP uplighter in the bottom-left corner for the whole room, its arm over the chair bed. No second floor lamp fits without narrowing the 41.4" walkway or standing where the opened chair bed goes.
(3) The wall anchor is the PJÄTTERYD set, three 22" canvases (68") on the left wall over the chair bed and the plant, which the run faces when you turn around. No art hangs over the run itself, where the four screens cover the wall.
(4) The rug's natural tone moves up into the sand of the PJÄTTERYD and the TRAVSPORT's oak effect; the gray-green repeats in the VALLKRASSING cushion, the guest's ÄNGSLILJA duvet cover and the monstera; the white of the desks, the BESTÅ and the HOPPVALS blinds ties the rest. Every textile here is washable. The FREIVID standing mat is out (final audit 2026-10-26): polyester over foam, not plastic-free; a cork standing mat is to find.
(5) The FEJKA monstera stands by the wardrobe. An artificial plant gathers dust, so it is wiped with the dusting.
Clearances are as in a (F-31, F-32, F-33, F-35, F-62). The usable floor runs from y 12 (furniture) and y 18 (upholstery) off the heater wall to y 116 at the wardrobe's landing and y 122 at the door's. desk-1 is 7.6" from the heater face. desk-3 is 6" from it, with 47" behind it and 3/4" to spare beside its monitor and the ELLOVEN (47.25 − 28 − 18.5, F-62). The personal run has 47" to the work desk. The BESTÅ hangs above the work desk, as in c. The chair bed is 5.5" above the wardrobe's landing, with a 41.4" walkway between it and desk-2, where the way in passes behind the desk (tight:41.4 against the 42" for passing, F-35; assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, heater-depth-assumed).
Audit 2026-10-24: the plan stands, with two notes added. The work desk's window end is 12" from the window wall and chair-2 beside it rolls, so Maddie could climb to the window: fit window stops or guards there (not in the catalog; the sill is unmeasured, F-38). Opened, the chair bed's foot goes under desk-2, so nothing stands under that desk (a PC tower goes under desk-1), and desk-2 stays raised and unplugged while a guest sleeps, so nobody lowers it onto the bed.
2026-10-25: the KOLON ($24.99) gave way to the bamboo mat ($111.59). Total $3,093.12, prices as of 2026-10-22 and 2026-10-23 and 2026-10-25 (those before today are price-stale, F-02).
Final audit 2026-10-26, for every office layout: the HUANUO arms are C-clamps on the desks' back edges, and every run desk stands flush against its wall, so either the desk stands off the wall by the clamp's depth (not on the listing: measure the arm; at 2" the way in past the L's return becomes 31.8" in e and g, still over 30") or the arms use their grommet mount (the UPLIFT is configured with two grommets). No outlets are mapped: three motors, five screens, a PC and the lamps need a power strip in each cable tray (the MITTZON page offers KOPPLA, not captured) with a slack loop for the desk's 25" of travel, on the left wall or the right wall above the door. The wardrobe's left door, open 90°, stands on its hinge line at x 10, 1/2" from the TÅGARP and 1 1/2" from the plant's pot: fit a 90° stop on that door, and measure the room door's width and the wardrobe's distance from the right corner (the door as drawn, opened flat, lands 2" over the wardrobe's end). The way from the door to the job desk and the window passes the parked chair at the run (or the L) with 13 to 18 1/2" to spare, so the 30" walkway holds with the chairs pushed in.
Final audit 2026-10-26, this layout: the chair bed moved 3.9" toward the window, centered on desk-2's span, so its opened foot passes between the MITTZON's legs (it was 1.6" from the desk's end, where a T-foot stands); the floor BESTÅ gave way to the wall-hung 47 1/4" over the work desk; one 60 x 48 bamboo mat lies across the aisle under both chairs; the run has its own lamp; the FREIVID is out.

</details>

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Alternative for Andrew's office brief (2026-10-25: "add consifugraiton to office using vari L desk or multiple vari straight ones (bamboo), largest htat can fit"), meeting F-62 as e does with UPLIFT. The usable floor as in a and e: furniture from y 12 off the hatched heater wall (F-33, heater-depth-assumed), nothing in front of the wardrobe below y 116 or the door below y 122 (F-31: the 36" landings, assumed-position:closet and assumed-position:door). No desk stands along the window wall, so no screen faces the glass (F-38).
Why the 80x60 and not Vari's 80x80: an 80" wing across this 106" room leaves 26" to the far wall, under a 30" walkway (F-30), and that gap is the way in from the door; the only wall that takes a wing without it is the window wall, where the screens would face the glass (F-38). The 80x60's 60" return leaves 46". Vari sells no bamboo top (vari.com's own search for "bamboo" finds nothing); every desk here is in Light Wood, its nearest finish, pale like bamboo.
desk-l Vari L-Shape Electric Standing Desk 80x60, Light Wood/Slate (25-50 1/2"): rot 270, the 80" main along the left wall (x 0-27.5, y 36-116), the 60" return at its south end (x 27.5-60, y 88.5-116), on the user's left. The personal station: three 32" screens across the main on monitor arms (the outer two angled in, about 77") and the fourth on the return; three face east across the window and the fourth, on the return, north: when Andrew turns to it the window is directly behind him, 35" and 32" inside its ends on the line through his chair, so that screen turns about 30° toward the right wall on its arm, its reflection on that wall, or the blinds drop when it is bright (final audit 2026-10-26). F-31: its south end on the wardrobe's 36" landing line (y 116); F-38: it starts 36" below the window wall, so nothing stands in front of the window · fits.
desk-1 Vari ComfortEdge 48x30, Light Wood/Slate: right wall, rot 90, x 76-106, y 12-60, the separate job station, the laptop on the ELLOVEN beside one 32" monitor, with 1 1/2" to spare (48 − 28 − 18.5, F-62; a single arm for the monitor above the laptop would free 20", not in the catalog); 6" from the heater face (F-33) · fits. A 72" here (as first staged) left 16.6" between its end and the L's return, the only way in.
chair-1 FLINTAN in the L's corner, x 31-59, y 56-84, facing the main; it swivels to the return. chair-2 FLINTAN at the job desk, x 46.5-74.5, y 22-50, 16" from the heater wall; the chairs sit 6" apart, so both stations work at once (F-35: 48.5" from the main's front to the job desk). To reach the job desk you pass chair-1: 17" with it pulled out, about 30" with it tucked under the main.

lamp-1 TÅGARP: bottom-left corner, left of the wardrobe's landing. plant-1 FEJKA: top-left corner, above the L's north end.
rug-1 LOHALS (rug): x 30-82, y 22-99, under both chairs (6" under the job desk's front: level its feet). rug-2 Anji Mountain bamboo chair mat 48 x 42 (rug): x 28-76, y 50-92, under chair-1, plastic-free in place of the KOLON (its last 3 1/2" under the return's front). rug-3 Anji Mountain bamboo chair mat 48 x 36" (rug): x 27-75, y 13-49, under chair-2 at the job desk (final audit 2026-10-26: chair-2 rolled on the jute).
No guest bed: the L's return takes the left wall's south end and the right wall's is the way in, as in e.
Not placed: the Vari 80x80 L and the 72x30 job desk (above); the KOLON (plastic; Andrew wants mats that aren't).

Staged pick, re-reviewed 2026-10-23 for F-63, re-planned the same day for F-31's 36" landings, after Andrew asked of the TROFAST on the closet doors' arc: "why would a storage be put right in front of the door like this?", and re-staged 2026-10-26 on his IKEA cart: the KURA reversible bed in place of the MINNEN (f keeps the MINNEN room), with the ÅSVANG twin mattress, which is not in the cart, and the FLISAT desk, which is. The KURA is 7/8" wider and longer than the extended MINNEN, so the GODISHUS moves 1/2" and the TROFAST 7/8" to the right, and the BLODFLÄDER moves to the right-lower wall. The dresser, desk and BESTÅ unit stay. The floor in front of the door and the closet (y 86-122) is now open: the TROFAST moves to the bed's open side, and the book display, which stood 2.5" past the door's arc, comes off the plan because no wall is left for it. 13 placed, 9 extras.
bed-1 KURA (41 3/8 x 78 3/8", 45 5/8" high) in its LOW position only (F-21: no loft bed before 6; IKEA recommends the loft for 6 and older), head on the top wall, long side on the left wall, flush (IKEA: under 2 1/2" or over 9" from a wall): the frame above the mattress makes a den, and its rails invite climbing, so the canopy and the BESTÅ unit hang well above them. F-34 open side clear for 32.5" between the dresser and the TROFAST and 40" out to the desk chair, but the way into that space passes between the desk's front-left corner (67.75, 25.6) and the TROFAST's top corner (58.75, 49.4): 25.4" on the diagonal (walkway-narrow:25.4, F-30; 30" can't be had with the dresser and the TROFAST both on this side); the TROFAST runs along the lower 29" of its side and 7.6" past its foot; F-33 bedding 70.6" from the heater face.
dresser-1 GODISHUS 3-drawer beside the bed's head as the bedside table (23 5/8" high): F-32 32.5" in front to the TROFAST's end, 40" to the desk chair; F-21 anchor it (IKEA: "must be securely anchored").
desk-1 FLISAT on the top wall in the window corner: F-33 right end 6" from the heater face; F-35 30" chair zone clear behind it (y 25.6 to 55.6), the chair 19.5" from the heater, not backed against a heater wall; 2 5/8" from the dresser. chair-1 SUNDVIK pulled out at the desk ("Last chance" at IKEA: buy it with the desk).
cabinet-1 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors, 47 1/4x16 1/2x15" (top, new): top wall above the desk, x 58.75-106, its right end 6" short of the heater face (F-33): closed storage hung above her reach for markers, paint, glue and rotation toys a parent hands down (F-63): solid LAPPVIKEN doors (no glass, F-21), hung on 2 BESTÅ suspension rails screwed into studs (extras), underside about 54" up. The canvas art moves off this wall to make room.
toys-1 TROFAST along the bed's open side, rot 270, x 41.4-58.75, y 49.4-86: its back to the KURA's rail, its end 7.6" past the bed's foot, the boxes facing the play floor. F-32 53.25" in front of the boxes to the heater face; F-31 36" from the closet doors, its end on the edge of the closet's and the door's 36" landings without entering them; F-33 53.25" from the heater face, with no slot beside the heater. Her books stand face out on its top, within reach of the bed. Making the bed means reaching over it at the foot.
rug-1 VALLABY road-map rug, rot 0, x 61-100, y 40-92: the play floor in front of the TROFAST, 12" from the heater face (F-33), clear of the desk chair and the TROFAST, so nothing stands on its edge; its last 6" lie in the closet's landing, 2.5" short of the doors' arc.
canopy-1 LÖVA on the top wall over the KURA's head (the den is the reading nook, the TROFAST's books at hand); mount it well above the 45 5/8" frame, out of her reach (IKEA). lamp-1 UPPLYST on the left wall inside the den as her reading light, cord fastened to the wall (IKEA). lamp-2 RÖDFLIK desk lamp (top) at the desk's left end, x 68.5-74.5, is her task light, the window on her right, so her drawing hand casts no shadow (none before the final audit 2026-10-26); its cord fastened along the top wall to an outlet (where the outlets are is still open). nightlight-1 TÖVÄDER and plant-1 FEJKA (artificial) on the dresser. art-1 BLODFLÄDER (canvas, no glass, F-21) on the right-lower wall facing the play floor, x 103, y 96-112.5, its bottom about 30" up, at her eye level; off the left wall, where it hung above the MINNEN's footboard: over the KURA's 45 5/8" rails it would hang out of sight and within reach from the mattress. No door swings on that wall (the door is at the bottom-left corner, F-07).
Maddie is 4 (F-21): every piece within her reach is under 27" or anchored (the GODISHUS carries IKEA's anchor warning at 23 5/8", so it is anchored too); no glass anywhere; no loft or bunk (the KURA stays low until she is 6).
Walkways (F-30): the 36" landings in front of the door and the closet are open floor (F-31); door to room along them and up past the TROFAST's end, on the landings' edge; bed to desk 40"; into the bed's side 25.4" between the desk's corner and the TROFAST's corner (walkway-narrow:25.4); bed to door around the TROFAST's end.
Extras: ÅSVANG mattress (4 3/4", under the KURA's 5 1/8" maximum; roll-packed, so no F-22 flag) and DVALA fitted sheet in the frame, SMÅSPORRE insert, BARNDRÖM cover, low pillow, SKOGSDUVA cushions x2, BENGTA panel (cut to clear the heater, F-33) on the HUGAD rod (assumed-position:window), BESTÅ suspension rails x2. Not placed: the FLISAT book display ikea-80614566 (above).
Total $1,280.31: placed $982.88 + extras $297.43; prices as of 2026-10-22 and 2026-10-23 (price-stale, F-02). Flags across the layout: assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, heater-depth-assumed, walkway-narrow:25.4 (the way into the bed's side).
Audit 2026-10-24 (from the rug-free E): two safety lines. The desk ends 12" from the window wall beside the window and her chair is a step onto it, so fit window stops or guards before move-in (not in the catalog; the sill is unmeasured, F-38). Standing on the desk she can open the BESTÅ cabinet hung above it, so give it a child lock or keep only washable, non-toxic supplies in it.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the way into the bed's open side measured 18.2" between the desk's front-left corner and the TROFAST's top corner, narrower than the 24" it led into (the notes' 41" ran past that corner), so the TROFAST moved 8.5" toward the foot, its end on the landings' edge: the way in is 25.7" and the side 32.5"; the art hangs 4" higher, above the footboard, to clear a door up to 35.5"; a RÖDFLIK at the desk's left end is her task light, with the window on her right; the VIMSIG's delivery flag was listed (gone with the KURA's roll-packed ÅSVANG). The MINNEN is out: Andrew's cart has the KURA (f keeps the MINNEN room).

</details>

bed-1 MINNEN (fully extended) along the top wall, head on the left wall, flush on both: F-34 its open side faces the room, clear for 24" between the dresser and the TROFAST (x 16.9 to 40.9); the TROFAST runs along its foot half; F-33 bedding (the foot) 34.5" from the heater face.
dresser-1 GODISHUS on the left wall at the bed's head, the bedside table; drawers face the room: F-32 24" clear (x 16.9 to 40.9, open floor); F-21 anchor it (IKEA).
table-1 LÄTT on the top wall in the window corner, x 80-104.75, 2.5" past the bed's foot: the drawing table gets the daylight. F-33 right end 7.25" from the heater face; its two chairs sit side by side at its front (y 19 to 30) at x 78-89 and 89-100, so the right one keeps 12" from the heater's face (F-35, the chair side of a child's desk; until the final audit 2026-10-26 they sat at x 82.5-104.5, the right one 7.5" from it, and the left one clears the bed's foot by 0.5"); not under the window (13" in from the right wall). lamp-2 RÖDFLIK desk lamp (top) at its left end is her task light, the window on her right (none before the final audit 2026-10-26); its cord fastened along the top wall to an outlet.
cabinet-1 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors, 47 1/4x16 1/2x15" (top, new): left wall above the dresser and the book display, rot 270, x 0-16.5, y 41-88.25, starting at the bed's head edge, never over the bed; its lower end at y 88.25 is 33.75" from the door's hinge side and hangs 2.25" over the landing at 54 to 69" up, so it clears the open door only if the door is under 33.75" wide (the door is unmeasured and Andrew can't tape it before the move, 2026-10-26, F-07: her sketch's arc is centered on the bottom-left corner, so the hinge is there, and a bedroom door in a 1955 house runs 28" to 32", so it is taken as under 33.75" until move-in day's tape; the cabinet can't move toward the top wall without hanging over the bed's head, final audit 2026-10-26): closed storage, 30.4" above the GODISHUS's top, so standing on the dresser she reaches it: put a child lock on its push-open doors, or keep only washable, non-toxic supplies in it; for markers, paint, glue and rotation toys a parent hands down (F-63): solid LAPPVIKEN doors (no glass, F-21), hung on 2 BESTÅ suspension rails screwed into studs (extras), underside about 54" up. art-1 BLODFLÄDER (canvas, no glass, F-21) hangs between the book display and the cabinet.
toys-1 TROFAST along the bed's open side, rot 0, x 40.9-77.5, y 40.5-57.9: its back to the bed rail, its end flush with the bed's foot, the boxes facing the play floor. F-32 28" of open floor in front of the boxes before the closet's landing; F-31 64" from the closet doors and clear of both 36" landings; F-33 34.5" from the heater face. Making the bed means reaching over it along the foot half.
books-1 FLISAT book display on the left wall below the dresser, rot 270, x 0-11, y 65.5-84.75, covers facing the room: beside the door, not in front of it, 1.25" short of the door's 36" landing (F-31) and 7.25" from the open door's edge; the SKOGSDUVA floor cushions (extras) go in front of it. Flag: assumed-position:door.
rug-1 VALLABY rot 90, x 33-85, y 40.5-79.5: the play floor from the bed's rail out past the TROFAST, which stands wholly on it, so no foot sits on its edge; 27" from the heater face (F-33); clear of both landings.
canopy-1 LÖVA on the top wall over the bed's head, mounted out of her reach (IKEA): the bed is the reading nook, the book display a step away. lamp-1 UPPLYST on the left wall over the bed's head, cord fastened. nightlight-1 TÖVÄDER and plant-1 FEJKA (artificial) on the dresser.
Maddie is 4 (F-21): every piece within her reach is under 27" or anchored (the GODISHUS carries IKEA's anchor warning at 23 5/8", so it is anchored too); no glass anywhere; no loft or bunk. The play table ends 13.25" from the window wall beside the window, with its 11" chairs as a step up: fit window stops or guards on her window before she moves in (assumed-position:window, F-38; the sill is unmeasured).
Walkways (F-30): the 36" landings in front of the door and the closet are open floor (F-31); door to room straight in past the book display's end; bed to play table around the TROFAST's end and up the 34.5" between the bed's foot and the heater face.

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Rug-free alternative (F-66): the staged arrangement (a) kept piece for piece, the KURA since Andrew's cart of 2026-10-26, without the VALLABY rug and the LÖVA canopy, for a family that may skip rugs for dust and dust mites. 11 placed, 9 extras.
What replaced the rug: nothing on the floor. The play floor between the TROFAST and the desk stays bare and wipes clean, and her two SKOGSDUVA flower cushions (machine-washable at 40 °C) are her seats there. The GREJSIMOJS mat does not fit open: it is 47 1/4" square, and the free floor between the TROFAST's boxes (x 57.9) and the heater's 12" for textiles (x 100, F-33) is 42 1/8" wide, with the door's and the closet's 36" landings below it (F-31). It folds up into a toy box, but its page gives no folded size, so it is not drawn here (F-11); it is staged open in the living room's e. No plastic-free mat fits either (Andrew, 2026-10-25: "something that isn't literally plastic"): her play floor is 48 x 30" for a mat that isn't a textile (6" off the heater's face), and the cork mats on natural rubber come 72 to 80" long; the foam mats that would fit are plastic.
F-66 moves: (1) the bed as the anchor: the KURA on the left wall, the GODISHUS beside its head as the bedside table and the TROFAST along its foot, her reading nook; (2) light: the UPPLYST cloud above the KURA's head end, where the canopy hung, the TÖVÄDER cat night light on the GODISHUS, and the ceiling light, which marks the play floor if it is central as most are (its place isn't recorded), with no floor lamp on the play floor, which a 4-year-old would knock over; (3) no wall anchor over the bed, on purpose: nothing hangs over a 4-year-old's bed that she could pull down (the BLODFLÄDER's own note), so the cloud lamp crowns the bed's head end and the BLODFLÄDER two birds (canvas, no glass) hangs at her eye level on the right-lower wall, facing the play floor; (4) color off the floor in washable textiles: the BARNDRÖM flower bedding (cover and fitted sheet, machine wash at 140°F/60°C, hot enough to kill dust mites), the SMÅSPORRE insert (also 140°F/60°C) and the SKOGSDUVA cushions, with the green of the FLISAT desk and the FEJKA; (5) the FEJKA on the GODISHUS (artificial: wipe it); (6) no mat (above), the one F-66 move this room can't make. The LÖVA canopy is left out: it gathers dust and washes only by hand.
bed-1 KURA (41 3/8 x 78 3/8", 45 5/8" high) in its LOW position only (F-21: no loft bed before 6; IKEA recommends the loft for 6 and older), head on the top wall, long side on the left wall, flush (IKEA: under 2 1/2" or over 9" from a wall): the frame above the mattress makes a den, and its rails invite climbing, so the canopy and the BESTÅ unit hang well above them. F-34 open side clear for 32.5" between the dresser and the TROFAST and 40" out to the desk chair, but the way into that space passes between the desk's front-left corner (67.75, 25.6) and the TROFAST's top corner (58.75, 49.4): 25.4" on the diagonal (walkway-narrow:25.4, F-30; 30" can't be had with the dresser and the TROFAST both on this side); the TROFAST runs along the lower 29" of its side and 7.6" past its foot; F-33 bedding 70.6" from the heater face.
dresser-1 GODISHUS 3-drawer beside the bed's head as the bedside table (23 5/8" high): F-32 32.5" in front to the TROFAST's end; F-21 anchor it (IKEA: "must be securely anchored").
desk-1 FLISAT on the top wall in the window corner: F-33 right end 6" from the heater face; F-35 30" chair zone clear behind it (y 25.6 to 55.6), the chair 19.5" from the heater, not backed against a heater wall; 2 5/8" from the dresser. chair-1 SUNDVIK pulled out at the desk ("Last chance" at IKEA: buy it with the desk).
cabinet-1 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors, 47 1/4x16 1/2x15" (top): top wall above the desk, x 58.75-106, its right end 6" short of the heater face (F-33): closed storage for markers, paint, glue and rotation toys a parent hands down (F-63): solid LAPPVIKEN doors (no glass, F-21), hung on 2 BESTÅ suspension rails screwed into studs (extras), underside about 54" up. That is above her reach from the floor only: standing on the desk she reaches it, so put a child lock on its push-open doors, or keep only washable, non-toxic supplies in it.
toys-1 TROFAST along the bed's open side, rot 270, x 41.4-58.75, y 49.4-86: its back to the KURA's rail, its end 7.6" past the bed's foot, the boxes facing the play floor. F-32 53.25" in front of the boxes to the heater face; F-31 36" from the closet doors, its end on the edge of the closet's and the door's 36" landings without entering them; F-33 53.25" from the heater face, with no slot beside the heater. 20 1/2" tall with no anchoring warning on its page. Her books stand face out on its top, within reach of the bed. Making the bed means reaching over it at the foot.
lamp-1 UPPLYST cloud (top): moved from the left wall beside the middle of the bed (y 34-46, past her lap when she sits up) to the top wall above the KURA's head end, centered on the bed, x 14.25-26.25, above its 45 5/8" frame: light from over her shoulder when she sits up for a story, and its cord fastened down behind the frame rather than down the wall beside her. It gives 110 lumens, a glow: a story needs the ceiling light on too, and a brighter wall reading lamp (about 300 to 450 lumens, warm white, cord fastened) is not in the catalog. lamp-2 RÖDFLIK desk lamp (top) at the desk's left end, x 68.5-74.5, is her task light, the window on her right (none before the final audit 2026-10-26); its cord fastened along the top wall to an outlet. nightlight-1 TÖVÄDER (battery, no cord at the bed, timer) and plant-1 FEJKA (artificial) on the dresser. art-1 BLODFLÄDER (canvas, no glass, F-21) on the right-lower wall facing the play floor, x 103, y 96-112.5, its bottom about 30" up, at her eye level; off the left wall, where it hung above the MINNEN's footboard: over the KURA's 45 5/8" rails it would hang out of sight and within reach from the mattress. No door swings on that wall (the door is at the bottom-left corner, F-07).
Maddie is 4 (F-21): every piece within her reach is under 27" or anchored (the GODISHUS carries IKEA's anchor warning at 23 5/8", so it is anchored too); no glass anywhere; no loft or bunk (the KURA stays low until she is 6). The desk ends 12" from the window wall, beside the window, with the chair as a step up to it: fit window stops or guards on her window before she moves in (assumed-position:window). The heater's type is unknown: if its cover gets hot, guard the 6" slot at the desk's end, where crayons fall.
Walkways (F-30): the 36" landings in front of the door and the closet are open floor (F-31); door to room along them and up past the TROFAST's end, on the landings' edge; bed to desk 40"; into the bed's side 25.4" between the desk's corner and the TROFAST's corner (walkway-narrow:25.4); bed to door around the TROFAST's end.
Extras: ÅSVANG mattress (4 3/4", under the KURA's 5 1/8" maximum; roll-packed, so no F-22 flag) and DVALA fitted sheet in the frame, SMÅSPORRE insert, BARNDRÖM cover, low pillow, SKOGSDUVA cushions x2 (40 °C doesn't kill dust mites; wash them often), BENGTA panel (cut to clear the heater, F-33) on the HUGAD rod (assumed-position:window), BESTÅ suspension rails x2. Not placed: the FLISAT book display ikea-80614566 (as in a), the VALLABY rug and the LÖVA canopy (F-66, above), the GREJSIMOJS (it does not fit open, above).
Total $1,235.33: placed $937.90 + extras $297.43; prices as of 2026-10-22 and 2026-10-23 (price-stale, F-02). Flags across the layout: assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, heater-depth-assumed, walkway-narrow:25.4 (the way into the bed's side).
Audit 2026-10-24: without the rug and canopy it is still a child's room (the flower bedding, the cloud lamp, the cat light, the green desk and the two-birds print carry it), plainer than a, and the bare play floor is the real loss. The UPPLYST moved above the headboard: a crown where the canopy hung, light over her shoulder, its cord behind the headboard. The notes no longer claim a lamp marking the play floor or a wall anchor over the bed. Two safety lines added: window stops, and a lock on the BESTÅ, since the desk is a step up to both.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the way into the bed's open side measured 18.2" between the desk's front-left corner and the TROFAST's top corner, narrower than the 24" it led into (the notes' 41" ran past that corner), so the TROFAST moved 8.5" toward the foot, its end on the landings' edge: the way in is 25.7" and the side 32.5"; the art hangs 4" higher, above the footboard, to clear a door up to 35.5"; a RÖDFLIK at the desk's left end is her task light, with the window on her right; the VIMSIG's delivery flag was listed (gone with the KURA's roll-packed ÅSVANG). The MINNEN is out: Andrew's cart has the KURA (f keeps the MINNEN room).

</details>

<details><summary>Why it's laid out this way</summary>

Alternative since 2026-10-26: the room as staged with the MINNEN before Andrew's IKEA cart chose the KURA (a).
Staged until 2026-10-26, re-reviewed 2026-10-23 for F-63 and re-planned the same day for F-31's 36" landings, after Andrew asked of the TROFAST on the closet doors' arc: "why would a storage be put right in front of the door like this?" The bed, dresser, desk and BESTÅ unit stay. The floor in front of the door and the closet (y 86-122) is now open: the TROFAST moves to the bed's open side, and the book display, which stood 2.5" past the door's arc, comes off the plan because no wall is left for it. 13 placed, 9 extras.
bed-1 MINNEN (fully extended, 77.5"), head on the top wall, long side on the left wall, flush (IKEA: under 2 1/2" or over 9" from a wall): F-34 open side clear for 32.5" between the dresser and the TROFAST and 41" out to the desk chair, but the way into that space passes between the desk's front-left corner (67.75, 25.6) and the TROFAST's top corner (57.9, 49.4): 25.7" on the diagonal (walkway-narrow:25.7, F-30; it was 18.2" until the final audit 2026-10-26 moved the TROFAST 8.5" toward the foot, and 30" can't be had with the dresser and the TROFAST both on this side); the TROFAST runs along the lower 28" of its side and 8.5" past its foot; F-33 bedding 71.5" from the heater face.
dresser-1 GODISHUS 3-drawer beside the bed's head as the bedside table (23 5/8" high): F-32 32.5" in front to the TROFAST's end, 41" to the desk chair; F-21 anchor it (IKEA: "must be securely anchored").
desk-1 FLISAT on the top wall in the window corner: F-33 right end 6" from the heater face; F-35 30" chair zone clear behind it (y 25.6 to 55.6), the chair 19.5" from the heater, not backed against a heater wall; 3 1/8" from the dresser. chair-1 SUNDVIK pulled out at the desk ("Last chance" at IKEA: buy it with the desk).
cabinet-1 BESTÅ shelf unit with doors, 47 1/4x16 1/2x15" (top, new): top wall above the desk, x 58.75-106, its right end 6" short of the heater face (F-33): closed storage hung above her reach for markers, paint, glue and rotation toys a parent hands down (F-63): solid LAPPVIKEN doors (no glass, F-21), hung on 2 BESTÅ suspension rails screwed into studs (extras), underside about 54" up. The canvas art moves off this wall to make room.
toys-1 TROFAST along the bed's open side, rot 270, x 40.5-57.9, y 49.4-86: its back to the bed rail, its end 8.5" past the bed's foot, the boxes facing the play floor. F-32 54" in front of the boxes to the heater face; F-31 36" from the closet doors, its end on the edge of the closet's and the door's 36" landings without entering them; F-33 54" from the heater face, with no slot beside the heater. Her books stand face out on its top, within reach of the bed. Making the bed means reaching over it at the foot.
rug-1 VALLABY road-map rug, rot 0, x 61-100, y 40-92: the play floor in front of the TROFAST, 12" from the heater face (F-33), clear of the desk chair and the TROFAST, so nothing stands on its edge; its last 6" lie in the closet's landing, 2.5" short of the doors' arc.
canopy-1 LÖVA on the top wall over the bed's head (the bed is the reading nook, the TROFAST's books at hand); mount it out of her reach (IKEA). lamp-1 UPPLYST on the left wall over the bed as her reading light, cord fastened to the wall (IKEA). lamp-2 RÖDFLIK desk lamp (top) at the desk's left end, x 68.5-74.5, is her task light, the window on her right, so her drawing hand casts no shadow (none before the final audit 2026-10-26); its cord fastened along the top wall to an outlet (where the outlets are is still open). nightlight-1 TÖVÄDER and plant-1 FEJKA (artificial) on the dresser. art-1 BLODFLÄDER (canvas, no glass, F-21) on the left wall at the bed's foot, y 70-86.5, its bottom above the 28 3/8" footboard: it clears an open door up to 35.5" wide (at y 74-90.5 it cleared only a 30" door, by 1.5"; the door is unmeasured and can't be taped before the move, Andrew 2026-10-26, F-07: its hinge is at the bottom-left corner, as the sketch's arc shows, and a bedroom door in a 1955 house runs 28" to 32", under the 35.5"; final audit 2026-10-26).
Maddie is 4 (F-21): every piece within her reach is under 27" or anchored (the GODISHUS carries IKEA's anchor warning at 23 5/8", so it is anchored too); no glass anywhere; no loft or bunk.
Walkways (F-30): the 36" landings in front of the door and the closet are open floor (F-31); door to room along them and up past the TROFAST's end, on the landings' edge; bed to desk 41"; into the bed's side 25.7" between the desk's corner and the TROFAST's corner (walkway-narrow:25.7); bed to door around the TROFAST's end.
Extras: VIMSIG mattress and BARNDRÖM fitted sheet in the frame, SMÅSPORRE insert, BARNDRÖM cover, low pillow, SKOGSDUVA cushions x2, BENGTA panel (cut to clear the heater, F-33) on the HUGAD rod (assumed-position:window), BESTÅ suspension rails x2. Not placed: the FLISAT book display ikea-80614566 (above).
Total $1,204.30: placed $832.88 + extras $371.42; prices as of 2026-10-22 (price-stale, F-02) except the BESTÅ pieces, read 2026-10-23. Flags across the layout: assumed-position:door, assumed-position:closet, assumed-position:window, heater-depth-assumed, walkway-narrow:25.7 (the way into the bed's side), delivery-path-unmeasured (the VIMSIG mattress ships flat, F-22).
Audit 2026-10-24 (from the rug-free E): two safety lines. The desk ends 12" from the window wall beside the window and her chair is a step onto it, so fit window stops or guards before move-in (not in the catalog; the sill is unmeasured, F-38). Standing on the desk she can open the BESTÅ cabinet hung above it, so give it a child lock or keep only washable, non-toxic supplies in it.
Final audit 2026-10-26: the way into the bed's open side measured 18.2" between the desk's front-left corner and the TROFAST's top corner, narrower than the 24" it led into (the notes' 41" ran past that corner), so the TROFAST moved 8.5" toward the foot, its end on the landings' edge: the way in is 25.7" and the side 32.5"; the art hangs 4" higher, above the footboard, to clear a door up to 35.5"; a RÖDFLIK at the desk's left end is her task light, with the window on her right; the VIMSIG's delivery flag is listed. The MINNEN's page gives 50 to 77 1/2" (the catalog, read 2026-10-22): re-read it before buying.

</details>
